# 大乘阿毘達磨集論

> [Ed: 282 segment(s) have no counterpart in the Tibetan and carry no transclusion: 3-886, 3-887, 3-888, 3-889, 3-890, 3-891, 3-892, 3-893 …. The two witnesses disagree on segmentation — resolve before relying on the alignment.]

## 大乘阿毘達磨集論 — 1/4 ^1-0

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1]]

云何世間？ ^1-1

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2]]

幾是世間？ ^1-2

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-3]]

為何義故觀世間耶？ ^1-3

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-4]]

謂三界所攝， ^1-4

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-5]]

及出世智後所得，似彼顯現，是世間義。 ^1-5

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-6]]

諸蘊一分， ^1-6

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-7]]

十五界、 ^1-7

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-8]]

十處全、 ^1-8

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-9]]

及三界、 ^1-9

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-10]]

二處一分，是世間。 ^1-10

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-11]]

為捨執著世依我故，觀察世間。 ^1-11

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-12]]

云何出世？ ^1-12

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-13]]

幾是出世？ ^1-13

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-14]]

為何義故觀出世耶？ ^1-14

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-15]]

謂能對治三界， ^1-15

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-16]]

無顛倒、無戲論、無分別故， ^1-16

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-17]]

是無分別出世間義。 ^1-17

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-19]]

又出世後所得，亦名出世。 ^1-19

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-20]]

依止出世故，是出世義。 ^1-20

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-21]]

諸蘊一分， ^1-21

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-22]]

及三界、 ^1-22

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-23]]

二處一分，是出世。 ^1-23

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-24]]

為捨執著獨存我故，觀察出世。 ^1-24

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-25]]

_云何已生？ ^1-25

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-26]]

幾是已生？ ^1-26

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-27]]

為何義故觀已生耶？ ^1-27

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-28]]

謂過去、現在，是已生義。 ^1-28

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-29]]

一切一分是已生。 ^1-29

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-30]]

為捨執著非常我故，觀察已生。 ^1-30

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-31]]

又有二十四種已生， ^1-31

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-32]]

謂最初已生、 ^1-32

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-33]]

相續已生、 ^1-33

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-34]]

長養已生、 ^1-34

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-35]]

依止已生、 ^1-35

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-36]]

轉變已生、 ^1-36

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-37]]

成熟已生、 ^1-37

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-38]]

退墮已生、 ^1-38

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-39]]

勝進已生、 ^1-39

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-40]]

清淨已生、 ^1-40

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-41]]

不清淨已生、 ^1-41

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-42]]

運轉已生、 ^1-42

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-43]]

有種已生、 ^1-43

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-44]]

無種已生、 ^1-44

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-45]]

影像自在示現已生、 ^1-45

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-46]]

展轉已生、 ^1-46

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-47]]

剎那壞已生、 ^1-47

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-48]]

離會已生、 ^1-48

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-49]]

異位已生、 ^1-49

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-50]]

生死已生、 ^1-50

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-51]]

成壞已生、 ^1-51

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-52]]

先時已生、 ^1-52

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-53]]

死時已生、 ^1-53

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-54]]

中時已生、 ^1-54

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-55]]

續時已生。 ^1-55

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-56]]

_云何非已生？ ^1-56

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-57]]

幾是非已生？ ^1-57

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-58]]

為何義故觀非已生耶？ ^1-58

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-59]]

謂未來及無為法，是非已生義。 ^1-59

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-60]]

一切一分是非已生。 ^1-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-61]]

為捨執著常住我故，觀察非已生。 ^1-61

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-62]]

又已生相違，是非已生義。 ^1-62

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-63]]

_云何能取？ ^1-63

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-64]]

幾是能取？ ^1-64

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-65]]

為何義故觀能取耶？ ^1-65

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-66]]

謂諸色根、 ^1-66

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-67]]

及心心所，是能取義。 ^1-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-68]]

三蘊全， ^1-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-69]]

色、行蘊一分， ^1-69

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-70]]

十二界、 ^1-70

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-71]]

六處全， ^1-71

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-72]]

及法界、法處一分，是能取。 ^1-72

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-73]]

為捨執著能受用我故，觀察能取。 ^1-73

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-74]]

又能取有四種，謂不至能取、至能取、 ^1-74

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-75]]

自相現在各別境界能取、 ^1-75

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-76]]

自相、共相一切時一切境界能取。 ^1-76

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-77]]

又由和合識等生故，假立能取。 ^1-77

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-78]]

_云何所取？ ^1-78

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-79]]

幾是所取？ ^1-79

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-80]]

為何義故觀所取耶？ ^1-80

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-81]]

謂諸能取亦是所取。 ^1-81

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-82]]

或有所取非能取， ^1-82

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-83]]

謂唯是取所行義。 ^1-83

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-84]]

一切皆是所取。 ^1-84

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-85]]

為捨執著境界我故，觀察所取。 ^1-85

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-86]]

_云何外門？ ^1-86

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-87]]

幾是外門？ ^1-87

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-88]]

為何義故觀外門耶？ ^1-88

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-89]]

謂欲界所繫法是外門義。 ^1-89

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-90]]

除依佛教所生聞思慧 ^1-90

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-91]]

及彼隨法行所攝心心所等， ^1-91

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-92]]

{謂欲界所繫法是外門義。} ^1-92

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-93]]

四界、二處全 ^1-93

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-94]]

及餘一分欲界所攝，是外門。 ^1-94

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-95]]

為捨執著不離欲我故，觀察外門。 ^1-95

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-96]]

_云何內門？ ^1-96

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-97]]

幾是內門？ ^1-97

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-98]]

為何義故觀內門耶？ ^1-98

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-99]]

謂外門相違，是內門義。 ^1-99

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-100]]

除四界二處全及餘一分，是內門。 ^1-100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-101]]

為捨執著離欲我故，觀察內門。 ^1-101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-102]]

_云何染污？ ^1-102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-103]]

幾是染污？ ^1-103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-104]]

為何義故觀染污耶？ ^1-104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-105]]

謂不善及有覆無記法，是染污義。 ^1-105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-106]]

有覆無記者， ^1-106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-107]]

謂遍行意相應煩惱等， ^1-107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-109]]

及色無色界繫諸煩惱等。 ^1-109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-110]]

諸蘊、十界、四處一分，是染污。 ^1-110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-111]]

為捨執著煩惱合我故，觀察染污。 ^1-111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-112]]

_云何不染污？ ^1-112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-113]]

幾是不染污？ ^1-113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-114]]

為何義故觀不染污耶？ ^1-114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-115]]

謂善及無覆無記法，是不染污義。 ^1-115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-117]]

八界、八處全， ^1-117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-118]]

諸蘊及餘界處一分，是不染污。 ^1-118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-119]]

為捨執著離煩惱我故，觀察不染污。 ^1-119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-120]]

_云何過去？ ^1-120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-121]]

幾是過去？ ^1-121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-122]]

為何義故觀過去耶？ ^1-122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-123]]

謂自相已生已滅故、 ^1-123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-124]]

因果已受用故、 ^1-124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-125]]

染淨功用已謝故、 ^1-125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-126]]

攝因已壞故、 ^1-126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-127]]

果及自相有非有故、 ^1-127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-128]]

憶念分別相故、 ^1-128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-129]]

戀為雜染相故、 ^1-129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-130]]

捨為清淨相故，是過去義。 ^1-130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-131]]

一切一分是過去。 ^1-131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-132]]

為捨執著流轉我故，觀察過去。 ^1-132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-133]]

_云何未來？ ^1-133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-134]]

幾是未來？ ^1-134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-135]]

為何義故觀未來耶？ ^1-135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-136]]

謂有因非已生故、 ^1-136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-137]]

未得自相故、 ^1-137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-138]]

因果未受用故、 ^1-138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-139]]

雜染清淨性未現前故、 ^1-139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-140]]

因及自相有非有故、 ^1-140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-141]]

希為雜染相故、 ^1-141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-142]]

不希為清淨相故， 是未來義。 ^1-142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-143]]

一切一分是未來。 ^1-143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-144]]

為捨執著流轉我故，觀察未來。 ^1-144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-145]]

_云何現在？ ^1-145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-146]]

幾是現在？ ^1-146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-147]]

為何義故觀現在耶？ ^1-147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-148]]

謂自相已生未滅故、 ^1-148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-149]]

因果受用未受用故、 ^1-149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-150]]

雜染清淨正現前故、 ^1-150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-151]]

能顯過去未來相故、 ^1-151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-152]]

作用現前故，是現在義。 ^1-152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-153]]

一切一分是現在。 ^1-153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-154]]

為捨執著流轉我故，觀察現在。 ^1-154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-155]]

_何故過去、未來、現在說名言事， ^1-155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-156]]

非涅槃等？ ^1-156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-157]]

內自所證、不可說故、 ^1-157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-158]]

唯曾、當、現，是言說所依故。 ^1-158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-159]]

_云何善？ ^1-159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-160]]

幾是善？ ^1-160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-161]]

為何義故觀善耶？ ^1-161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-162]]

謂自性故、 ^1-162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-163]]

相屬故、 ^1-163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-164]]

隨逐故、 ^1-164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-165]]

發起故、 ^1-165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-166]]

勝義故、 ^1-166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-167]]

生得故、 ^1-167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-168]]

加行故、 ^1-168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-169]]

現前供養故、 ^1-169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-170]]

饒益故、 ^1-170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-171]]

引攝故、 ^1-171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-172]]

對治故、 ^1-172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-173]]

寂靜故、 ^1-173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-174]]

等流故， ^1-174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-175]]

是善義。 ^1-175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-176]]

五蘊、十界、四處一分是善。 ^1-176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-177]]

為捨執著法合我故， 觀察善。 ^1-177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-178]]

何等自性善？ ^1-178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-179]]

謂信等十一心所有法。 ^1-179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-180]]

何等相屬善？ ^1-180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-181]]

謂彼相應法。 ^1-181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-182]]

何等隨逐善？ ^1-182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-183]]

謂即彼諸法習氣。 ^1-183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-184]]

何等發起善？ ^1-184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-185]]

謂彼所發身業語業。 ^1-185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-186]]

何等勝義善？ ^1-186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-187]]

謂真如。 ^1-187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-188]]

何等生得善？ ^1-188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-189]]

謂即彼諸善法，由先串習故， ^1-189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-190]]

感得如是報， ^1-190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-191]]

由此自性，即於是處，不由思惟任運樂住。 ^1-191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-192]]

何等加行善。 ^1-192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-193]]

謂依止親近善丈夫故， ^1-193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-194]]

聽聞正法 ^1-194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-195]]

如理作意， ^1-195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-196]]

修習淨善法隨法行。 ^1-196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-197]]

何等現前供養善？ ^1-197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-198]]

謂想對如來， ^1-198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-199]]

建立靈廟圖寫尊容； ^1-199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-200]]

或想對正法，書治法藏興供養業。 ^1-200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-201]]

何等饒益善？ ^1-201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-202]]

謂以四攝事，饒益一切有情。 ^1-202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-203]]

何等引攝善？ ^1-203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-204]]

謂施性福業事 ^1-204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-205]]

及戒性福業事故， ^1-205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-206]]

引攝生天樂異熟、 ^1-206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-207]]

引攝生富貴家、 ^1-207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-208]]

引攝隨順清淨法。 ^1-208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-209]]

何等對治善？ ^1-209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-210]]

謂厭壞對治、 ^1-210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-211]]

斷對治、 ^1-211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-212]]

持對治、 ^1-212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-213]]

遠分對治、 ^1-213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-214]]

伏對治、 ^1-214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-215]]

離繫對治、 ^1-215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-216]]

煩惱障對治、 ^1-216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-217]]

所知障對治。 ^1-217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-218]]

何等寂靜善？ ^1-218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-219]]

謂永斷貪欲、 ^1-219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-220]]

永斷瞋恚、 ^1-220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-221]]

永斷愚癡、 ^1-221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-222]]

永斷一切煩惱， ^1-222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-223]]

若想、受滅、 ^1-223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-224]]

若有餘依涅槃界、 ^1-224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-225]]

若無餘依涅槃界、 ^1-225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-226]]

若無所住涅槃界。 ^1-226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-227]]

何等等流善？ ^1-227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-228]]

謂已得寂靜者，由此增上力故， ^1-228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-229]]

發起勝品神通等世、出世共不共功德。 ^1-229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-230]]

_云何不善？ ^1-230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-231]]

幾是不善？ ^1-231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-232]]

為何義故觀不善耶？ ^1-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-233]]

謂自性故、 ^1-233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-234]]

相屬故、 ^1-234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-235]]

隨逐故、 ^1-235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-236]]

發起故、 ^1-236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-237]]

勝義故、 ^1-237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-238]]

生得故、 ^1-238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-239]]

加行故、 ^1-239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-240]]

現前供養故、 ^1-240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-241]]

損害故、 ^1-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-242]]

引攝故、 ^1-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-243]]

所治故、 ^1-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-244]]

障礙故， 是不善義。 ^1-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-245]]

五蘊、十界、四處一分，是不善。 ^1-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-246]]

為捨執著非法合我故，觀察不善。 ^1-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-247]]

何等自性不善？ ^1-247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-248]]

謂除染污意相應，及色、無色界煩惱等， ^1-248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-249]]

所餘能發惡行煩惱隨煩惱。 ^1-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-250]]

何等相屬不善？ ^1-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-251]]

謂即此煩惱、隨煩惱相應法。 ^1-251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-252]]

何等隨逐不善？ ^1-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-253]]

謂即彼習氣。 ^1-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-254]]

何等發起不善？ ^1-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-255]]

謂彼所起身業、語業。 ^1-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-256]]

何等勝義不善？ ^1-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-257]]

謂一切流轉。 ^1-257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-258]]

何等生得不善？ ^1-258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-259]]

謂由串習不善故，感得如是異熟， ^1-259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-260]]

由此自性，即於不善任運樂住。 ^1-260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-261]]

何等加行不善？ ^1-261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-262]]

謂依止親近不善丈夫故， ^1-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-263]]

聽聞不正法， ^1-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-264]]

不如理作意行 ^1-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-265]]

身、語、意惡行。 ^1-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-266]]

何等現前供養不善？ ^1-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-267]]

謂想對歸依隨一天眾已， ^1-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-268]]

或殺害意為先、或邪惡意為先， ^1-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-269]]

建立祠廟廣興供養業， 令無量眾廣樹非福。 ^1-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-270]]

何等損害不善？ ^1-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-271]]

謂於一切處，起身、語、意種種邪行。 ^1-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-272]]

何等引攝不善？ ^1-272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-273]]

謂行身、語、意諸惡行已， ^1-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-274]]

於惡趣、善趣，引攝不愛果異熟，或引或滿。 ^1-274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-275]]

何等所治不善？ ^1-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-276]]

謂諸對治所對治法。 ^1-276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-277]]

何等障礙不善？ ^1-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-278]]

謂能障礙諸善品法。 ^1-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-279]]

_云何無記？ ^1-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-280]]

幾是無記？ ^1-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-281]]

為何義故觀無記耶？ ^1-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-282]]

謂自性故、 ^1-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-283]]

相屬故、 ^1-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-284]]

隨逐故、 ^1-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-285]]

發起故、 ^1-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-286]]

勝義故、 ^1-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-287]]

生得故、 ^1-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-288]]

加行故、 ^1-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-289]]

現前供養故、 ^1-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-290]]

饒益故、 ^1-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-291]]

受用故、 ^1-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-292]]

引攝故、 ^1-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-293]]

對治故、 ^1-293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-294]]

寂靜故、 ^1-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-295]]

等流故，是無記義。 ^1-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-296]]

八界、八處全， ^1-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-297]]

及餘蘊、界、處一分，是無記。 ^1-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-298]]

為捨執著離法非法我故，觀察無記。 ^1-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-299]]

何等自性無記？ ^1-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-300]]

謂八色界、處， ^1-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-301]]

意相應品， ^1-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-302]]

命根、 ^1-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-303]]

眾同分、 ^1-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-304]]

名、 ^1-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-305]]

句、 ^1-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-306]]

文身等。 ^1-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-307]]

何等相屬無記？ ^1-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-308]]

謂懷非穢 ^1-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-309]]

非淨心者，所有由名、句、文身所攝受心心所法。 ^1-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-310]]

何等隨逐無記？ ^1-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-311]]

謂即彼戲論習氣。 ^1-311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-312]]

何等發起無記？ ^1-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-313]]

謂彼所攝受諸心心所法，所發身業、語業。 ^1-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-314]]

何等勝義無記？ ^1-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-315]]

謂虛空、非擇滅。 ^1-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-316]]

何等生得無記？ ^1-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-317]]

謂諸不善、 ^1-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-318]]

有漏善法異熟。 ^1-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-319]]

何等加行無記？ ^1-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-320]]

謂非染、 ^1-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-321]]

非善心者所有威儀路、工巧處法。 ^1-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-322]]

何等現前供養無記？ ^1-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-323]]

謂如有一想對歸依隨一天眾， ^1-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-324]]

遠離殺害意邪惡見， ^1-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-325]]

建立祠廟興供養業， 令無量眾於如是處，不生長福非福。 ^1-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-326]]

何等饒益無記？ ^1-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-327]]

謂如有一於自僕使妻子等所， ^1-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-328]]

以非穢、非淨心而行惠施。 ^1-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-329]]

何等受用無記？ ^1-329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-330]]

謂如有一以無簡擇、 ^1-330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-331]]

無染污心受用資具。 ^1-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-332]]

何等引攝無記？ ^1-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-333]]

謂如有一於工巧處串習故， ^1-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-334]]

於當來世，復引攝如是相身， ^1-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-335]]

由此身故，習工巧處速疾究竟。 ^1-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-336]]

何等對治無記？ ^1-336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-337]]

謂如有一為治疾病得安樂故，以簡擇心好服醫藥。 ^1-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-338]]

何等寂靜無記？ ^1-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-339]]

謂色、無色界諸煩惱等， ^1-339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-340]]

由奢摩他所藏伏故。 ^1-340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-341]]

何等等流無記？ ^1-341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-342]]

謂變化心俱生品。 ^1-342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-343]]

_復有示現善、不善、無記法。 ^1-343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-344]]

此復云何？ ^1-344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-346]]

謂佛及得第一究竟菩薩摩訶薩，為欲饒益諸有情故，有所示現。 ^1-346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-347]]

當知此中，無有一法真實可得。 ^1-347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-348]]

_云何欲界繫？ ^1-348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-349]]

幾是欲界繫？ ^1-349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-350]]

為何義故觀欲界繫耶？ ^1-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-351]]

謂未離欲者所有善、 ^1-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-352]]

不善、無記法，是欲界繫義。 ^1-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-353]]

四界二處全， ^1-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-354]]

及餘蘊、界、處一分，是欲界繫。 ^1-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-355]]

為捨執著未離欲界欲我故，觀察欲界繫。 ^1-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-356]]

_云何色界繫？ ^1-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-357]]

幾是色界繫？ ^1-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-358]]

為何義故觀色界繫耶？ ^1-358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-359]]

謂已離欲界欲、 ^1-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-361]]

未離色界欲者， 所有善、無記法，是色界繫義。 ^1-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-362]]

除前所說四界二處，餘蘊界處一分，是色界繫。 ^1-362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-363]]

為捨執著離欲界欲我故，觀察色界繫。 ^1-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-364]]

_云何無色界繫？ ^1-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-365]]

幾是無色界繫？ ^1-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-366]]

為何義故觀無色界繫耶？ ^1-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-367]]

謂已離色界欲？ ^1-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-368]]

未離無色界欲者，所有善無記法，是無色界繫義。 ^1-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-369]]

三界、二處、四蘊一分，是無色界繫。 ^1-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-370]]

為捨執著離色界欲我故，觀察無色界繫。 ^1-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-371]]

_復次有一分離欲、 具分離欲、 ^1-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-372]]

通達離欲、 ^1-372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-373]]

損伏離欲、 ^1-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-374]]

永害離欲。 ^1-374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-376]]

復有十種離欲， ^1-376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-377]]

謂自性離欲、 ^1-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-378]]

損害離欲、 ^1-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-379]]

任持離欲、 ^1-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-380]]

增上離欲、 ^1-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-381]]

愚癡離欲、 ^1-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-382]]

對治離欲、 ^1-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-383]]

遍知離欲、 ^1-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-384]]

永斷離欲、 ^1-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-385]]

有上離欲、 ^1-385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-386]]

無上離欲。 ^1-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-387]]

何等自性離欲？ ^1-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-388]]

謂於苦受及順苦受處法生厭背性。 ^1-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-389]]

何等損害離欲？ ^1-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-390]]

謂習欲者暢熱惱已，生厭背性。 ^1-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-391]]

何等任持離欲？ ^1-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-392]]

謂飽食已，於諸美膳生厭背性。 ^1-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-393]]

何等增上離欲？ ^1-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-394]]

謂得勝處已， ^1-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-395]]

於下劣處生厭背性。 ^1-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-396]]

何等愚癡離欲？ ^1-396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-397]]

謂諸愚夫於涅槃界生厭背性。 ^1-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-398]]

何等對治離欲？ ^1-398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-399]]

謂由世間、出世間道，斷諸煩惱。 ^1-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-400]]

何等遍知離欲？ ^1-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-401]]

謂已得見道者？ ^1-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-402]]

於三界法生厭背性。 ^1-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-403]]

何等永斷離欲？ ^1-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-404]]

謂永斷地地諸煩惱已，生厭背性。 ^1-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-405]]

何等有上離欲？ ^1-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-406]]

謂世間、聲聞、獨覺所有離欲。 ^1-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-407]]

何等無上離欲？ ^1-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-408]]

謂佛菩薩所有離欲， ^1-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-409]]

為欲利樂諸有情故。 ^1-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-410]]

_云何有學？ ^1-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-411]]

幾是有學？ ^1-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-412]]

為何義故觀有學耶？ ^1-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-413]]

謂求解脫者所有善法，是有學義。 ^1-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-414]]

十界、四處、諸蘊一分，是有學。 ^1-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-415]]

為捨執著求解脫我故，觀察有學。 ^1-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-416]]

_云何無學？ ^1-416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-417]]

幾是無學？ ^1-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-418]]

為何義故觀無學耶？ ^1-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-419]]

謂於諸學處已得究竟者所有善法，是無學義。 ^1-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-421]]

為捨執著已脫我故，觀察無學。 ^1-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-422]]

_云何非學非無學？ ^1-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-423]]

幾是非學非無學？ ^1-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-424]]

為何義故觀非學非無學耶？ ^1-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-425]]

謂諸異生所有善、 ^1-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-426]]

不善無記法， ^1-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-427]]

及諸學者染污、 ^1-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-428]]

無記法、 ^1-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-429]]

諸無學者無記法、 ^1-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-430]]

并無為法，是非學非無學義。 ^1-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-431]]

八界、 ^1-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-432]]

八處全， ^1-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-433]]

及餘蘊界處一分，是非學非無學。 ^1-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-434]]

為捨執著不解脫我故，觀察非學非無學。 ^1-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-435]]

_云何見所斷？ ^1-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-436]]

幾是見所斷？ ^1-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-437]]

為何義故觀見所斷耶？ ^1-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-438]]

謂分別所起染污見疑見、 ^1-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-439]]

處疑處，及於見等所起邪行煩惱隨煩惱， ^1-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-440]]

及由見等所發身、語、意業， ^1-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-441]]

并一切惡趣等蘊、界、處，是見所斷義。 ^1-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-442]]

一切一分是見所斷。 ^1-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-443]]

為捨執著見圓滿我故，觀察見所斷。 ^1-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-444]]

_云何修所斷？ ^1-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-445]]

幾是修所斷？ ^1-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-446]]

為何義故觀修所斷耶？ ^1-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-447]]

謂得見道後，見所斷相違諸有漏法，是修所斷義。 ^1-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-448]]

一切一分是修所斷。 ^1-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-449]]

為捨執著修圓滿我故，觀察修所斷。 ^1-449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-450]]

_云何非所斷？ ^1-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-451]]

幾是非所斷？ ^1-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-452]]

為何義故觀非所斷耶？ ^1-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-453]]

謂諸無漏法， ^1-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-454]]

除順決擇分，是非所斷。 ^1-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-455]]

十界四處諸蘊一分，是非所斷。 ^1-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-456]]

為捨執著成滿我故，觀察非所斷。 ^1-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-457]]

_云何緣生？ ^1-457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-458]]

幾是緣生？ ^1-458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-459]]

為何義故觀緣生耶？ ^1-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-460]]

謂相故、 ^1-460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-461]]

分別支故、 ^1-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-462]]

略攝支故、 ^1-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-463]]

建立支緣故、 ^1-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-464]]

建立支業故、 ^1-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-465]]

支雜染攝故、 ^1-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-466]]

義故、 ^1-466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-467]]

甚深故、 ^1-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-468]]

差別故、 ^1-468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-469]]

順、逆故， 是緣生義。 一切皆是緣生， ^1-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-470]]

唯除法界、法處一分諸無為法。 ^1-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-471]]

為捨執著無因不平等因我法故，觀察緣生。 ^1-471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-472]]

_何等相故？ ^1-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-473]]

謂無作緣生故、 ^1-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-474]]

無常緣生故、 ^1-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-475]]

勢用緣生故， 是緣生相。 ^1-475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-476]]

_何等分別支故？ ^1-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-477]]

謂分別緣生為十二分。 ^1-477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-478]]

何等十二？ ^1-478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-479]]

謂無明、 ^1-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-480]]

行、 ^1-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-481]]

識、 ^1-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-482]]

名色、 ^1-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-483]]

六處、 ^1-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-484]]

觸、 ^1-484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-485]]

受、 ^1-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-486]]

愛、 ^1-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-487]]

取、 ^1-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-488]]

有、 ^1-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-489]]

生 ^1-489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-490]]

及老死。 ^1-490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-491]]

_何等略攝支故？ ^1-491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-492]]

謂能引支、 ^1-492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-493]]

所引支、 ^1-493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-494]]

能生支、 ^1-494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-495]]

所生支。 ^1-495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-496]]

能引支者， ^1-496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-497]]

謂無明、 ^1-497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-498]]

行、 ^1-498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-499]]

識。 ^1-499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-500]]

所引支者， ^1-500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-501]]

謂名色、 ^1-501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-502]]

六處、 ^1-502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-503]]

觸、 ^1-503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-504]]

受。 ^1-504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-505]]

能生支者， ^1-505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-506]]

謂愛， ^1-506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-507]]

取， ^1-507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-508]]

有。 ^1-508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-509]]

所生支者， ^1-509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-510]]

謂生、 ^1-510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-511]]

老死。 ^1-511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-512]]

_何等建立支緣故？ ^1-512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-513]]

謂習氣故、 ^1-513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-514]]

引發故、 ^1-514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-515]]

思惟故、俱有故， ^1-515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-516]]

建立支緣，隨其所應。 ^1-516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-518]]

_何等建立支業故？ ^1-518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-519]]

謂無明有二種業： ^1-519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-520]]

一令諸有情於有愚癡； ^1-520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-521]]

二與行作緣。 ^1-521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-522]]

行有二種業： ^1-522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-523]]

一令諸有情於諸趣中種種差別； ^1-523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-524]]

二與識作緣，由熏習故。 ^1-524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-525]]

識有二種業： ^1-525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-526]]

一持諸有情所有業縛； ^1-526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-527]]

二與名色作緣。 ^1-527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-528]]

名色有二種業： ^1-528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-529]]

一攝諸有情自體； ^1-529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-530]]

二與六處作緣。 ^1-530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-531]]

六處有二種業： ^1-531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-532]]

一攝諸有情自體圓滿； ^1-532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-533]]

二與觸作緣。 ^1-533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-534]]

觸有二種業： ^1-534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-535]]

一令諸有情，於所受用境界流轉； ^1-535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-536]]

二與受作緣。 ^1-536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-537]]

受有二種業： ^1-537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-538]]

一令諸有情，於所受用生果流轉； ^1-538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-539]]

二與愛作緣。 ^1-539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-540]]

愛有二種業： ^1-540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-541]]

一引諸有情流轉生死； ^1-541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-542]]

二與取作緣。 ^1-542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-543]]

取有二種業： ^1-543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-544]]

一為取後有，令諸有情發有取識； ^1-544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-545]]

二與有作緣。 ^1-545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-546]]

有有二種業： ^1-546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-547]]

一令諸有情後有現前； ^1-547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-548]]

二與生作緣。 ^1-548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-549]]

生有二種業： ^1-549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-550]]

一令諸有情，名色、 六處、 ^1-550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-551]]

觸、受，次第生起； ^1-551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-552]]

二與老死作緣。 ^1-552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-553]]

老死有二種業： ^1-553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-554]]

一數令有情時分變異； ^1-554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-555]]

二數令有情壽命變異。 ^1-555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-556]]

_何等支雜染攝故？ ^1-556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-557]]

謂若無明、 ^1-557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-558]]

若愛、 ^1-558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-559]]

若取，是煩惱雜染所攝。 ^1-559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-560]]

若行、 ^1-560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-561]]

若識、 ^1-561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-562]]

若有，是業雜染所攝。 ^1-562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-563]]

餘是生雜染所攝。 ^1-563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-564]]

_何等義故？ ^1-564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-565]]

謂無作者義、 ^1-565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-566]]

有因義、 ^1-566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-567]]

離有情義、 ^1-567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-568]]

依他起義、 ^1-568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-569]]

無作用義、 ^1-569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-570]]

無常義、 ^1-570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-571]]

有剎那義、 ^1-571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-572]]

因果相續不斷義、 ^1-572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-573]]

因果相似攝受義、 ^1-573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-574]]

因果差別義、 ^1-574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-575]]

因果決定義，是緣起義。 ^1-575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-576]]

_何等甚深故？ ^1-576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-577]]

謂因甚深故、 ^1-577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-578]]

相甚深故、 ^1-578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-579]]

生甚深故、 ^1-579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-580]]

住甚深故、 ^1-580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-581]]

轉甚深故，是甚深義。 ^1-581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-582]]

又諸緣起法雖剎那滅， ^1-582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-583]]

而住可得， ^1-583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-584]]

雖無作用緣， ^1-584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-585]]

而有功能緣可得， ^1-585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-586]]

雖離有情而有情可得， ^1-586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-587]]

雖無作者， ^1-587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-588]]

而諸業果不壞可得，是故甚深。 ^1-588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-589]]

又諸緣起法，不從自生、 ^1-589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-590]]

不從他生、 ^1-590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-591]]

不從共生， ^1-591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-592]]

非不自作、 ^1-592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-593]]

他作、 ^1-593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-594]]

因生， ^1-594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-595]]

是故甚深。 ^1-595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-596]]

_何等差別故？ ^1-596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-597]]

謂識生差別故、 ^1-597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-598]]

內死生差別故、 ^1-598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-599]]

外穀等生差別故、 ^1-599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-600]]

成壞差別故、 ^1-600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-601]]

食持差別故、 ^1-601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-602]]

愛非愛趣分別差別故、 ^1-602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-603]]

清淨差別故、 ^1-603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-604]]

威德差別故，是差別義。 ^1-604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-605]]

_何等順逆故？ ^1-605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-606]]

謂雜染順逆故、 ^1-606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-607]]

清淨順逆故， ^1-607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-608]]

是說緣起順逆義。 ^1-608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-609]]

云何緣？ ^1-609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-610]]

幾是緣？ ^1-610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-611]]

為何義故觀緣耶？ ^1-611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-612]]

謂因故、等無間故、 ^1-612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-613]]

所緣故、增上故，是緣義。 ^1-613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-614]]

一切是緣。 ^1-614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-615]]

為捨執著我為因法故，觀察緣。 ^1-615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-616]]

何等因緣？ ^1-616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-617]]

謂阿賴耶識及善習氣。 ^1-617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-618]]

又自性故、 ^1-618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-619]]

差別故、 ^1-619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-620]]

助伴故、 ^1-620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-621]]

等行故、 ^1-621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-622]]

增益故、 ^1-622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-623]]

障礙故、 ^1-623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-624]]

攝受故，是因緣義。 ^1-624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-625]]

自性者， ^1-625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-626]]

謂能作因。 ^1-626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-627]]

自性差別者， ^1-627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-628]]

謂能作因差別，略有二十種。 ^1-628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-629]]

一生能作， ^1-629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-630]]

謂識和合望識。 ^1-630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-631]]

二住能作， ^1-631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-632]]

謂食望已生及求生有情。 ^1-632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-633]]

三持能作， ^1-633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-634]]

謂大地望有情。 ^1-634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-635]]

四照能作， ^1-635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-636]]

謂燈等望諸色。 ^1-636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-637]]

五變壞能作， ^1-637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-638]]

謂火望薪。 ^1-638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-639]]

六分離能作， ^1-639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-640]]

謂鎌等望所斷。 ^1-640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-641]]

七轉變能作， ^1-641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-642]]

謂工巧智等，望金銀等物。 ^1-642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-643]]

八信解能作， ^1-643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-644]]

謂煙望火。 ^1-644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-645]]

九顯了能作， ^1-645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-646]]

謂宗、因、喻，望所成義。 ^1-646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-647]]

十等至能作， ^1-647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-648]]

謂聖道望涅槃。 ^1-648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-649]]

十一隨說能作， ^1-649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-650]]

謂名想見。 ^1-650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-651]]

十二觀待能作， ^1-651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-652]]

謂觀待此故，於彼求欲生， ^1-652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-653]]

如待飢渴追求飲食。 ^1-653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-654]]

十三牽引能作， ^1-654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-655]]

謂懸遠緣， ^1-655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-656]]

如無明望老死。 ^1-656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-657]]

十四生起能作， ^1-657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-658]]

謂隣近緣， ^1-658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-659]]

如無明望行。 ^1-659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-660]]

十五攝受能作， ^1-660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-661]]

謂所餘緣， ^1-661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-662]]

如田、水、糞等，望穀生等。 ^1-662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-663]]

十六引發能作， ^1-663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-664]]

謂隨順緣， ^1-664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-665]]

如正事王，令王悅豫。 ^1-665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-666]]

十七定別能作， ^1-666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-667]]

謂差別緣， ^1-667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-668]]

如五趣緣，望五趣果。 ^1-668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-669]]

十八同事能作， ^1-669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-670]]

謂和合緣， ^1-670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-671]]

如根不壞， ^1-671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-672]]

境界現前， ^1-672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-673]]

作意正起，望所生識。 ^1-673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-674]]

十九相違能作， ^1-674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-675]]

謂障礙緣， ^1-675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-676]]

如雹望穀。 ^1-676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-677]]

二十不相違能作， ^1-677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-678]]

謂無障礙緣， ^1-678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-679]]

如穀無障。 ^1-679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-680]]

助伴者， ^1-680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-681]]

謂諸法共有，而生必無缺減， ^1-681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-682]]

如四大種及所造色，隨其所應。 ^1-682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-683]]

等行者， ^1-683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-684]]

謂諸法共有等行所緣 ^1-684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-685]]

必無缺減， ^1-685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-686]]

如心心所。 ^1-686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-687]]

增益者， ^1-687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-688]]

謂前際修善、不善、無記法故， ^1-688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-689]]

能令後際善等諸法，展轉增勝後後生起。 ^1-689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-690]]

障礙者， ^1-690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-691]]

謂隨所數習諸煩惱故，隨所有惑皆得相續增長堅固，乃令相續遠避涅槃。 ^1-691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-692]]

攝受者， ^1-692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-693]]

謂不善及善有漏法，能攝受自體故。 ^1-693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-694]]

_何等等無間緣？ ^1-694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-695]]

謂中無間隔，等無間故。 ^1-695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-696]]

同分異分心心所生，等無間故。 是等無間緣義。 ^1-696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-697]]

_何等所緣緣？ ^1-697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-698]]

謂有分齊境所緣故、 ^1-698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-699]]

無分齊境所緣故、 ^1-699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-700]]

無異行相境所緣故、 ^1-700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-701]]

有異行相境所緣故、 ^1-701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-702]]

有事境所緣故、 ^1-702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-703]]

無事境所緣故、 ^1-703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-704]]

事所緣故、 ^1-704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-705]]

分別所緣故、 ^1-705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-706]]

有顛倒所緣故、 ^1-706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-707]]

無顛倒所緣故、 ^1-707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-708]]

有礙所緣故、 ^1-708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-709]]

無礙所緣故，是所緣緣義。 ^1-709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-710]]

_何等增上緣？ ^1-710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-711]]

謂任持增上故、 ^1-711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-712]]

引發增上故、 ^1-712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-713]]

俱有增上故、 ^1-713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-714]]

境界增上故、 ^1-714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-715]]

產生增上故、 ^1-715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-716]]

住持增上故、 ^1-716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-717]]

受用果增上故、 ^1-717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-718]]

世間清淨離欲增上故、 ^1-718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-719]]

出世清淨離欲增上故，是增上緣義。 ^1-719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-720]]

_云何同分彼同分？ ^1-720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-721]]

幾是同分彼同分？ ^1-721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-722]]

為何義故觀同分彼同分耶？ ^1-722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-723]]

謂不離識彼相似根，於境相續生故， ^1-723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-724]]

離識自相似，相續生故，是同分彼同分義。 ^1-724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-725]]

色蘊一分、 ^1-725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-726]]

眼等五、有色界處一分，是同分彼同分。 ^1-726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-727]]

為捨執著與識相應不相應我故，觀察同分彼同分。 ^1-727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-728]]

_云何執受？ ^1-728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-729]]

幾是執受？ ^1-729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-730]]

為何義故觀執受耶？ ^1-730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-731]]

謂受生所依色故，是執受義。 ^1-731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-732]]

色蘊一分、 ^1-732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-733]]

五有色界、處全及四一分，是執受。 ^1-733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-734]]

為捨執著身自在轉我故，觀察執受。 ^1-734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-735]]

_云何根？ ^1-735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-736]]

幾是根？ ^1-736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-737]]

為何義故觀根耶？ ^1-737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-738]]

謂取境增上故、 ^1-738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-739]]

種族不斷增上故、 ^1-739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-740]]

眾同分住增上故、 ^1-740

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-741]]

受用淨不淨業果增上故、 ^1-741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-742]]

世間離欲增上故、 ^1-742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-743]]

出世離欲增上故，是根義。 ^1-743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-744]]

受、識蘊全、 ^1-744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-745]]

色行蘊一分、 ^1-745

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-746]]

十二界、六處全、 ^1-746

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-747]]

法界法處一分，是根， ^1-747

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-748]]

為捨執著增上我，故觀察根。 ^1-748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-749]]

_云何苦苦性？ ^1-749

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-750]]

幾是苦苦性？ ^1-750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-751]]

為何義故觀苦苦性耶？ ^1-751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-752]]

謂苦受自相故、 ^1-752

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-753]]

隨順苦受法自相故，是苦苦性義。 ^1-753

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-754]]

一切一分，是苦苦性。 ^1-754

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-755]]

為捨執著有苦我故，觀察苦苦性。 ^1-755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-756]]

_云何壞苦性？ ^1-756

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-757]]

幾是壞苦性？ ^1-757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-758]]

為何義故觀壞苦性耶？ ^1-758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-759]]

謂樂受變壞自相故、 ^1-759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-760]]

隨順樂受法變壞自相故、 ^1-760

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-761]]

於彼愛心變壞故，是壞苦性義。 ^1-761

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-762]]

一切一分，是壞苦性。 ^1-762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-763]]

為捨執著有樂我故，觀察壞苦性。 ^1-763

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-764]]

_云何行苦性？ ^1-764

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-765]]

幾是行苦性？ ^1-765

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-766]]

為何義故觀行苦性耶？ ^1-766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-767]]

謂不苦不樂受自相故、 ^1-767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-768]]

隨順不苦不樂受法自相故、 ^1-768

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-769]]

彼二麁重所攝受故， ^1-769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-770]]

不離二無常所隨， ^1-770

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-771]]

不安隱故，是行苦性義。 ^1-771

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-772]]

除三界、二處、諸蘊一分，一切是行苦性。 ^1-772

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-773]]

為捨執著有不苦不樂我故，觀察行苦性。 ^1-773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-774]]

_云何有異熟？ ^1-774

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-775]]

幾是有異熟？ ^1-775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-776]]

為何義故觀有異熟耶？ ^1-776

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-777]]

謂不善及善有漏，是有異熟。 ^1-777

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-778]]

十界、四處、諸蘊一分，是有異熟。 ^1-778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-779]]

為捨執著能捨能續諸蘊我故，觀察有異熟。 ^1-779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-780]]

又異熟者，唯阿賴耶識及相應法， ^1-780

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-781]]

餘但異熟生，非異熟。 ^1-781

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-782]]

_云何食？ ^1-782

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-783]]

幾是食？ ^1-783

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-784]]

為何義故觀食耶？ ^1-784

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-785]]

謂變壞故、 ^1-785

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-786]]

有變壞者境界故、 ^1-786

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-787]]

有境界者希望故、 ^1-787

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-788]]

有希望者取故，有取者是食義。 ^1-788

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-789]]

三蘊、十一界、五處一分，是食。 ^1-789

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-790]]

為捨執著由食住我故，觀察食。 ^1-790

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-791]]

又此食 ^1-791

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-792]]

差別建立，略有四種： ^1-792

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-793]]

一不淨依止住食、 ^1-793

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-794]]

二淨不淨依止住食、 ^1-794

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-795]]

三清淨依止住食、 ^1-795

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-796]]

四示現住食。 ^1-796

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-797]]

_云何有上？ ^1-797

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-798]]

幾是有上？ ^1-798

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-799]]

為何義故觀有上耶？ ^1-799

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-800]]

謂一切有為故、無為一分故，是有上義。 ^1-800

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-801]]

除法界、法處一分，一切是有上。 ^1-801

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-802]]

為捨執著下劣事我故，觀察有上。 ^1-802

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-803]]

_云何無上？ ^1-803

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-804]]

幾是無上？ ^1-804

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-805]]

為何義故觀無上耶？ ^1-805

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-806]]

謂無為一分故，是無上義。 ^1-806

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-807]]

法界、法處一分，是無上。 ^1-807

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-808]]

為捨執著最勝事我故，觀察無上。 ^1-808

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-809]]

_由此所說差別道理，餘無量門可類觀察。 ^1-809

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-810]]

復次蘊、界、處差別，略有三種： ^1-810

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-811]]

謂遍計所執相差別、 ^1-811

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-812]]

所分別相差別、 ^1-812

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-813]]

法性相差別。 ^1-813

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-814]]

何等遍計所執相差別？ ^1-814

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-815]]

謂於蘊、界、處中遍計所執我、 ^1-815

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-816]]

有情、 ^1-816

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-817]]

命者、 ^1-817

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-818]]

生者、 ^1-818

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-819]]

養者、 ^1-819

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-820]]

數取趣者、 ^1-820

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-821]]

意生者、 ^1-821

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-822]]

摩納婆等。 ^1-822

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-823]]

何等所分別相差別？ ^1-823

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-824]]

謂即蘊、界、處法。 ^1-824

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-825]]

何等法性相差別？ ^1-825

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-826]]

謂即於蘊界處中我等無性， ^1-826

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-827]]

無我有性。 ^1-827

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-828]]

_復有四種差別， ^1-828

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-829]]

謂相差別、 ^1-829

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-830]]

分別差別、 ^1-830

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-831]]

依止差別、 ^1-831

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-832]]

相續差別。 ^1-832

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-833]]

何等相差別？ ^1-833

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-834]]

謂蘊、界、處一一自相差別。 ^1-834

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-835]]

何等分別差別？ ^1-835

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-836]]

謂即於蘊、界、處中，實有、假有、 ^1-836

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-837]]

世俗有、勝義有、 ^1-837

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-838]]

有色、無色、 ^1-838

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-839]]

有見、無見， ^1-839

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-840]]

如是等無量差別，分別如前說。 ^1-840

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-841]]

何等依止差別？ ^1-841

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-842]]

謂乃至有情依止差別有爾所， ^1-842

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-843]]

當知蘊、界、處亦爾。 ^1-843

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-844]]

何等相續差別？ ^1-844

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-845]]

謂一一剎那，蘊、界、處轉。 ^1-845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-846]]

於相差別善巧，為何所了知？ ^1-846

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-847]]

謂了知我執過患。 ^1-847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-848]]

於分別差別善巧，為何所了知？ ^1-848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-849]]

謂了知聚想過患。 ^1-849

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-850]]

於依止差別善巧，為何所了知？ ^1-850

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-851]]

謂了知不作而得、 ^1-851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-852]]

雖作而失想過患。 ^1-852

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-853]]

於相續差別善巧，為何所了知？ ^1-853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-854]]

謂了知安住想過患。 ^1-854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-855]]

_又蘊、界、處有六種差別， ^1-855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-856]]

謂外門差別、 ^1-856

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-857]]

內門差別、 ^1-857

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-858]]

長時差別、 ^1-858

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-859]]

分限差別、 ^1-859

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-860]]

暫時差別、 ^1-860

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-861]]

顯示差別。 ^1-861

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-862]]

何等外門差別？ ^1-862

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-863]]

謂多分欲界差別。 ^1-863

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-864]]

何等內門差別？ ^1-864

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-865]]

謂一切定地。 ^1-865

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-866]]

何等長時差別？ ^1-866

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-867]]

謂諸異生。 ^1-867

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-868]]

何等分限差別？ ^1-868

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-869]]

謂諸有學，及除最後剎那蘊、界、處所餘無學。 ^1-869

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-870]]

何等暫時差別？ ^1-870

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-871]]

謂諸無學最後剎那蘊、界、處。 ^1-871

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-872]]

何等顯示差別？ ^1-872

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-873]]

謂諸佛及已得究竟菩薩摩訶薩所示現諸蘊、界、處。 ^1-873

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-874]]

_大乘阿毘達磨集論本事分中攝品第二_ ^1-874

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-875]]

云何攝？ ^1-875

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-876]]

略說攝有十一種， ^1-876

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-877]]

謂相攝、 ^1-877

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-878]]

界攝、 ^1-878

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-879]]

種類攝、 ^1-879

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-880]]

分位攝、 ^1-880

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-881]]

伴攝、 ^1-881

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-882]]

方攝、 ^1-882

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-883]]

時攝、 ^1-883

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-884]]

一分攝、 ^1-884

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-885]]

具分攝、 ^1-885

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-886]]

更互攝、 ^1-886

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-887]]

勝義攝。 ^1-887

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-888]]

_何等相攝？ ^1-888

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-889]]

謂蘊、界、處一一自相，即體自攝。 ^1-889

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-890]]

_何等界攝？ ^1-890

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-891]]

謂蘊、界、處所有種子阿賴耶識 ^1-891

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-892]]

能攝彼界。 ^1-892

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-893]]

_何等種類攝？ ^1-893

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-894]]

謂蘊、界、處其相雖異，蘊義、界義、處義等故，展轉相攝。 ^1-894

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-895]]

_何等分位攝？ ^1-895

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-896]]

謂樂位蘊、界、處，即自相攝。 ^1-896

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-897]]

苦位、不苦不樂位亦爾，分位等故。 ^1-897

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-898]]

_何等伴攝？ ^1-898

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-899]]

謂色蘊與餘蘊互為伴故，即攝助伴。 ^1-899

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-900]]

餘蘊、界、處亦爾。 ^1-900

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-901]]

_何等方攝？ ^1-901

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-902]]

謂依東方諸蘊、界、處，還自相攝， ^1-902

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-903]]

餘方蘊、界、處亦爾。 ^1-903

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-904]]

_何等時攝？ ^1-904

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-905]]

謂過去世諸蘊、界、處，還自相攝， ^1-905

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-906]]

未來、現在諸蘊、界、處亦爾。 ^1-906

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-907]]

_何等一分攝？ ^1-907

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-908]]

謂所有法，蘊、界、處所攝，但攝一分非餘，應知一分攝。 ^1-908

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-909]]

_何等具分攝？ ^1-909

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-910]]

謂所有法，蘊、界、處所攝，能攝全分，應知具分攝。 ^1-910

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-911]]

_何等更互攝？ ^1-911

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-912]]

謂色蘊攝幾界？幾處？ ^1-912

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-913]]

十全一少分。 ^1-913
