![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-914]]

受蘊攝幾界？幾處？ ^1-914

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-915]]

一少分。 ^1-915

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-916]]

如受蘊，想、行蘊亦爾。 ^1-916

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-917]]

識蘊攝幾界？幾處？ ^1-917

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-918]]

七界一處。 ^1-918

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-919]]

眼界攝幾蘊？幾處？ ^1-919

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-920]]

色蘊少分一處全。 ^1-920

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-921]]

如眼界， 耳、 鼻、舌、身。 ^1-921

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-923]]

色、聲、香、味、觸界亦爾。 ^1-923

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-924]]

意界攝幾蘊幾處？ ^1-924

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-925]]

一蘊一處。 ^1-925

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-926]]

{眼識界攝幾蘊幾處？} ^1-926

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-927]]

{識蘊意處少分。} ^1-927

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-928]]

{如眼識，耳、鼻、舌、身、意識界亦爾。} ^1-928

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-929]]

法界攝幾蘊？幾處？ ^1-929

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-930]]

三蘊全、色蘊少分、一處全。 ^1-930

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-931]]

眼識界攝幾蘊幾處？ ^1-931

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-932]]

識蘊意處少分。 ^1-932

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-933]]

如眼識，耳、鼻、舌、身、意識界亦爾。 ^1-933

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-934]]

眼處攝幾蘊？幾界？ ^1-934

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-935]]

色蘊少分、一界全。 ^1-935

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-936]]

如眼處，耳、鼻、舌、身、色、聲、香、味、觸處亦爾。 ^1-936

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-937]]

意處攝幾蘊？幾界？ ^1-937

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-938]]

一蘊、七界。 ^1-938

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-939]]

法處攝幾蘊？幾界？ ^1-939

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-940]]

三蘊全一少分、一界全。 ^1-940

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-941]]

如是諸餘法，以蘊、界、處名說， ^1-941

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-942]]

及餘非蘊、界、處名說， ^1-942

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-943]]

如實有、假有、 ^1-943

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-944]]

世俗有、勝義有、 ^1-944

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-945]]

所知、所識、所達、 ^1-945

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-946]]

有色、無色、 ^1-946

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-947]]

有見、無見，如是等如前所顯， ^1-947

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-948]]

隨其所應，與蘊、界、處更互相攝盡當知。 ^1-948

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-949]]

_何等勝義攝？ ^1-949

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-950]]

謂蘊、界、處真如所攝。 ^1-950

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-951]]

於攝善巧，得何勝利？ ^1-951

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-952]]

得於所緣略集勝利。 ^1-952

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-953]]

隨彼彼境，略聚其心，如是如是善根增勝。 ^1-953

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-954]]

_大乘阿毘達磨集論本事分中相應品第三_ ^1-954

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-955]]

云何相應？ ^1-955

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-956]]

略說相應有六種， ^1-956

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-957]]

謂不相離相應、 ^1-957

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-958]]

和合相應、 ^1-958

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-959]]

聚集相應、 ^1-959

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-960]]

俱有相應、 ^1-960

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-961]]

作事相應、 ^1-961

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-962]]

同行相應。 ^1-962

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-963]]

_何等不相離相應？ ^1-963

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-964]]

謂一切有方分色與極微處互不相離。 ^1-964

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-965]]

_何等和合相應？ ^1-965

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-966]]

謂極微已上，一切有方分色更互和合。 ^1-966

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-967]]

_何等聚集相應？ ^1-967

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-968]]

謂方分聚色展轉集會。 ^1-968

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-969]]

_何等俱有相應？ ^1-969

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-970]]

謂一身中，諸蘊、界、處， 俱時流轉 ^1-970

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-971]]

同生住滅。 ^1-971

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-972]]

_何等作事相應？ ^1-972

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-973]]

謂於一所作事展轉相攝， ^1-973

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-974]]

如二苾芻隨一所作，更互相應。 ^1-974

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-975]]

_何等同行相應？ ^1-975

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-976]]

謂心心所，於一所緣展轉同行。 ^1-976

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-977]]

此同行相應復有多義，謂他性相應非己性。 ^1-977

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-978]]

不相違相應，非相違。 ^1-978

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-979]]

同時相應，非異時。 ^1-979

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-980]]

同分界地相應，非異分界地。 ^1-980

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-981]]

復有一切遍行同行相應， ^1-981

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-982]]

謂受、想、思、觸、作意、識。 ^1-982

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-983]]

復有染污遍行同行相應， ^1-983

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-984]]

謂於染污、意四種煩惱。 ^1-984

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-985]]

復有非一切時同行相應， ^1-985

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-986]]

謂依止心，或時起信等善法、 ^1-986

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-987]]

或時起貪等煩惱、 隨煩惱法。 ^1-987

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-988]]

復有分位同行相應，謂與樂受諸相應法。 ^1-988

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-989]]

與苦受、不苦不樂受諸相應法。 ^1-989

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-990]]

復有無間同行相應， ^1-990

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-991]]

謂在有心位。 ^1-991

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-992]]

復有有間同行相應， ^1-992

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-993]]

謂無心定所間。 ^1-993

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-994]]

復有外門同行相應， ^1-994

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-995]]

謂多分欲界繫心心所。 ^1-995

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-996]]

復有內門同行相應， ^1-996

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-997]]

謂諸定地所有心心所。 ^1-997

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-998]]

復有曾習同行相應， ^1-998

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-999]]

謂諸異生所有心心所， ^1-999

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1000]]

及有學者一分心心所。 ^1-1000

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1001]]

復有未曾習同行相應， ^1-1001

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1002]]

謂出世間諸心心所， ^1-1002

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1003]]

及初後時，出世後所得諸心心所。 ^1-1003

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1004]]

於相應善巧，得何勝利？ ^1-1004

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1005]]

能善了悟唯依止心，有受、想等染淨諸法相應不相應義。 ^1-1005

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1006]]

由此了悟，即能捨離計我能受、能想、 能思、能念。 ^1-1006

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1007]]

染淨執著。 ^1-1007

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1008]]

又能善巧速入無我。 ^1-1008

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1009]]

_大乘阿毘達磨集論本事分中成就品第四_ ^1-1009

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1010]]

云何成就？ ^1-1010

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1011]]

謂成就相如前所說。 ^1-1011

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1012]]

此差別有三種，謂種子成就、 ^1-1012

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1013]]

自在成就、 ^1-1013

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1014]]

現行成就。 ^1-1014

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1015]]

_何等種子成就？ ^1-1015

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1016]]

謂若生欲界， ^1-1016

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1017]]

欲、色、無色界繫煩惱、隨煩惱， ^1-1017

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1018]]

由種子成就故成就，及生得善。 ^1-1018

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1019]]

若生色界， ^1-1019

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1020]]

欲界繫煩惱、隨煩惱，由種子成就故成就，亦名不成就。 ^1-1020

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1021]]

色、無色界繫煩惱、隨煩惱， ^1-1021

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1022]]

由種子成就故成就，及生得善。 ^1-1022

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1023]]

若生無色界， ^1-1023

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1024]]

欲色界繫煩惱、隨煩惱，由種子成就故成就。 亦名不成就， ^1-1024

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1025]]

無色界繫煩惱、隨煩惱， ^1-1025

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1026]]

由種子成就故成就，及生得善。 ^1-1026

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1027]]

若已得三界對治道， ^1-1027

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1028]]

隨如是如是品類對治已生，如此如此品類由種子成就得不成就； ^1-1028

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1029]]

隨如是如是品類對治未生，如此如此品類由種子成就故成就。 ^1-1029

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1030]]

_何等自在成就？ ^1-1030

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1031]]

謂諸加行善法。 ^1-1031

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1032]]

若世出世靜慮、解脫、三摩地、 ^1-1032

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1033]]

三摩鉢底等功德， ^1-1033

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1034]]

及一分無記法，由自在成就故成就。 ^1-1034

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1035]]

_何等現行成就？ ^1-1035

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1036]]

謂諸蘊界處法隨所現前，若善、若不善、 ^1-1036

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1037]]

若無記，彼由現行成就故成就。 ^1-1037

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1038]]

若已斷善者，所有善法由種子成就故成就， ^1-1038

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1039]]

亦名不成就。 ^1-1039

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1040]]

若非涅槃法一闡底迦，究竟成就雜染諸法， ^1-1040

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1041]]

由闕解脫因，亦名阿顛底迦， ^1-1041

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1042]]

以彼解脫得因必竟不成就故。 ^1-1042

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1043]]

_於成就善巧，得何勝利？ ^1-1043

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1044]]

能善了知諸法增減。 ^1-1044

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1045]]

知增減故， ^1-1045

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1046]]

於世興衰離決定想， ^1-1046

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1047]]

乃至能斷若愛、若恚。 ^1-1047

## 大乘阿毘達磨集論 — 2/4 ^2-0

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-1]]

_大乘阿毘達磨集論決擇分中諦品第一之一 ^2-1

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-2]]

_云何決擇？ ^2-2

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-3]]

略說決擇有四種， ^2-3

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-4]]

謂諦決擇、法決擇、得決擇、論議決擇。 ^2-4

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-5]]

_云何諦決擇？ ^2-5

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-6]]

謂四聖諦， ^2-6

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-7]]

苦諦、集諦、滅諦、道諦。 ^2-7

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-8]]

云何苦諦？ ^2-8

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-9]]

謂有情生及生所依處。 ^2-9

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-10]]

_何等有情生？ ^2-10

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-11]]

即有情世間。謂在那落迦、傍生、餓鬼、人、天趣中。 ^2-11

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-12]]

人謂東毘提訶、西瞿陀尼、南贍部洲、北俱盧洲。 ^2-12

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-13]]

天謂四大王眾天、三十三天、夜摩天、覩史多天、樂變化天、他化自在天， ^2-13

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-14]]

梵眾天、梵輔天、大梵天、 ^2-14

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-15]]

少光天、無量光天、極光淨天、 ^2-15

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-16]]

少淨天、無量淨天、遍淨天、 ^2-16

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-17]]

無雲天、福生天、廣果天、 ^2-17

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-18]]

無想有情天、無煩天、無熱天、 ^2-18

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-19]]

善現天、善見天、色究竟天， ^2-19

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-20]]

無邊空處天、無邊識處天、無所有處天、非想非非想處天。{諸有情生} ^2-20

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-21]]

何等生所依處？ ^2-21

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-22]]

即器世間。 ^2-22

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-23]]

謂水輪依風輪， ^2-23

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-24]]

地輪依水輪。 ^2-24

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-25]]

依此地輪，有蘇迷盧山、七金山、四大洲、八小洲、內海外海。 ^2-25

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-26]]

蘇迷盧山四外層級，四大王眾天、三十三天所居處別， ^2-26

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-27]]

外輪圍山虛空宮殿， ^2-27

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-28]]

若夜摩天、覩史多天、化樂天、他化自在天， ^2-28

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-29]]

及色界天所居處別，諸阿素洛所居處別。 ^2-29

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-30]]

及諸那落迦所居處別，謂熱那落迦、寒那落迦、孤獨那落迦。 ^2-30

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-31]]

及一分傍生餓鬼所居處別。 ^2-31

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-32]]

乃至一日一月周遍流光所照方處，名一世界， ^2-32

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-33]]

如是千世界中，有千日千月、千蘇迷盧山王、 ^2-33

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-34]]

千四大洲、 ^2-34

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-35]]

千四大王眾天、千三十三天、千夜摩天、千覩史多天、千樂變化天、千他化自在天、 ^2-35

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-36]]

千梵世天，如是總名小千世界。 ^2-36

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-37]]

千小千界總名第二中千世界， ^2-37

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-38]]

千中千界總名第三大千世界。 ^2-38

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-39]]

如此三千大千世界，總有大輪圍山周匝圍繞。 ^2-39

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-40]]

又此三千大千世界，同壞同成。 ^2-40

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-41]]

譬如天雨滴如車軸，無間無斷從空下注， ^2-41

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-42]]

如是東方無間無斷無量世界，或有將壞、或有將成、或有正壞、 ^2-42

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-43]]

或壞已住、 ^2-43

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-44]]

或有正成、 ^2-44

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-45]]

或成已住。 ^2-45

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-46]]

如於東方，乃至一切十方亦爾。 ^2-46

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-47]]

若有情世間、若器世間，業煩惱力所生故、業煩惱增上所起故，總名苦諦。 ^2-47

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-48]]

_復有清淨世界，非苦諦攝、 ^2-48

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-49]]

非業煩惱力所生故、非業煩惱增上所起故， ^2-49

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-50]]

然由大願清淨善根增上所引。 ^2-50

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-51]]

此所生處不可思議，唯佛所覺； ^2-51

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-52]]

尚非得靜慮者靜慮境界，況尋思者。 ^2-52

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-53]]

_復次苦相差別有八，謂生苦、 ^2-53

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-54]]

老苦、 ^2-54

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-55]]

病苦、 ^2-55

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-56]]

死苦、 ^2-56

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-57]]

怨憎會苦、 ^2-57

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-58]]

愛別離苦、 ^2-58

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-59]]

求不得苦， ^2-59

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-60]]

略攝一切五取蘊苦。 ^2-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-61]]

生何因苦？ ^2-61

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-62]]

眾苦所逼故、餘苦所依故。 ^2-62

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-63]]

老何因苦？ ^2-63

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-64]]

時分變壞苦故。 ^2-64

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-65]]

病何因苦？ ^2-65

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-66]]

大種變異苦故。 ^2-66

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-67]]

死何因苦？ ^2-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-68]]

壽命變壞苦故。 ^2-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-69]]

怨憎會何因苦？ ^2-69

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-70]]

合會生苦故。 ^2-70

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-71]]

愛別離何因苦？ ^2-71

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-72]]

別離生苦故。 ^2-72

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-73]]

求不得何因苦？ ^2-73

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-74]]

所悕不果生苦故。 ^2-74

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-75]]

略攝一切五取蘊何因苦？ ^2-75

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-76]]

麁重苦故。 ^2-76

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-77]]

如是八種略攝為六， ^2-77

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-78]]

{如是六種廣開為八} ^2-78

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-79]]

謂逼迫苦、轉變苦、合會苦、別離苦、所悕不果苦、麁重苦。 ^2-79

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-80]]

如是六種廣開為八，若六若八平等平等。 ^2-80

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-81]]

如說三苦，此中八苦，為三攝八、八攝三耶？ ^2-81

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-82]]

展轉相攝。 ^2-82

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-83]]

所謂生苦、老苦、病苦、死苦、怨憎會苦，能顯苦苦； ^2-83

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-84]]

愛別離苦、求不得苦，能顯壞苦； ^2-84

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-85]]

略攝一切五取蘊苦，能顯行苦。 ^2-85

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-86]]

如說二苦，謂世俗諦苦、勝義諦苦。 ^2-86

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-87]]

何者世俗諦苦？ 何者勝義諦苦？ ^2-87

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-88]]

謂生苦乃至求不得苦，是世俗諦苦。 ^2-88

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-89]]

略攝一切五取蘊苦，是勝義諦苦。 ^2-89

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-90]]

_云何苦諦共相？ ^2-90

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-91]]

謂無常相、苦相、空相、無我相。 ^2-91

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-92]]

何等無常相？ ^2-92

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-93]]

略有十二，謂非有相、壞滅相、變異相、別離相、 ^2-93

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-94]]

現前相、法爾相、剎那相、相續相、 ^2-94

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-95]]

病等相、種種心行轉相、 ^2-95

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-96]]

資產興衰相、器世成壞相。 ^2-96

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-97]]

_何等非有相？ ^2-97

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-98]]

謂蘊界處，於一切時，我我所性，常非有故。 ^2-98

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-99]]

_何等壞滅相？ ^2-99

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-100]]

謂諸行生已即滅，暫有還無故。 ^2-100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-101]]

何等變異相？ ^2-101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-102]]

謂諸行異生，由不相似相續轉故。 ^2-102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-103]]

_何等別離相？ ^2-103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-104]]

謂於諸行失增上力，或他所攝執為己有。 ^2-104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-105]]

_何等現前相？ ^2-105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-106]]

謂正處無常，由因隨逐，今受無常故。 ^2-106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-107]]

_何等法爾相？ ^2-107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-108]]

謂當來無常，由因隨逐，定當受故。 ^2-108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-109]]

_何等剎那相？ ^2-109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-110]]

謂諸行剎那，後必不住故。 ^2-110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-111]]

_何等相續相？ ^2-111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-112]]

謂無始時來，諸行生滅相續不斷故。 ^2-112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-113]]

_何等病等相？ ^2-113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-114]]

謂四大、時分、壽命變異故。 ^2-114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-115]]

_何等種種心行轉相？ ^2-115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-116]]

謂於一時起有貪心、或於一時起離貪心， ^2-116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-117]]

如是有瞋離瞋、 ^2-117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-118]]

有癡離癡、 ^2-118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-119]]

若合若散、若下若舉、若掉離掉、 ^2-119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-120]]

若不寂靜若寂靜、 ^2-120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-121]]

若定不定，如是等心行流轉故。 ^2-121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-122]]

_何等資產興衰相？ ^2-122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-123]]

謂諸興善，終歸衰變故。 ^2-123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-124]]

_何等器世成壞相？ ^2-124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-125]]

謂火、水、風三種。 ^2-125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-126]]

成壞有三災，頂謂第二、第三、第四靜慮。 ^2-126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-127]]

第四靜慮外宮殿等，雖無外災成壞，然彼諸天與宮殿等俱生俱滅，說有成壞。 ^2-127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-128]]

復有三種中劫， ^2-128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-129]]

所謂饑饉、疫病、刀兵。 ^2-129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-130]]

此小三災，劫究竟位方乃出現。 ^2-130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-131]]

謂世界成已，一中劫初唯減、一中劫後唯增，十八中劫亦增亦減， ^2-131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-132]]

二十中劫世界正壞， ^2-132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-133]]

二十中劫世界壞已住， ^2-133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-134]]

二十中劫世界正成， ^2-134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-135]]

二十中劫世界成已住。 ^2-135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-136]]

合此八十中劫為一大劫， ^2-136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-137]]

由此劫數，顯色、無色界諸天壽量， ^2-137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-138]]

如說以壽盡故、福盡故、業盡故，彼彼有情從彼彼處沒。 ^2-138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-139]]

云何壽盡？ ^2-139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-140]]

謂時死。 ^2-140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-141]]

云何福盡？ ^2-141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-142]]

謂非時死，即非福死。 ^2-142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-143]]

以彼有情貪著定味，福力減盡 ^2-143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-144]]

因此命終。 ^2-144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-145]]

云何業盡？ ^2-145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-146]]

謂順生受業、順後受業俱盡故死。 ^2-146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-147]]

_何等苦相？ ^2-147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-148]]

謂或三苦、或八苦、或六苦，廣說如前，是名苦相。 ^2-148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-149]]

何故經說若無常者即是苦耶？ ^2-149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-150]]

由二分無常為緣，苦相可了知故。 ^2-150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-152]]

謂生分無常為緣，苦苦性可了知故； ^2-152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-153]]

滅分無常為緣，壞苦性可了知故； ^2-153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-154]]

俱分無常為緣，行苦性可了知故。 ^2-154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-155]]

即依此義，薄伽梵說：諸行無常、諸行變壞。 又依此義言，諸所有受我說皆苦。 ^2-155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-156]]

又於生滅二法所隨諸行中，有生等八苦， ^2-156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-157]]

性可了知故，佛說言：若無常者即是苦。 又於無常諸行中有生等苦可了知者，如來依此密意說言： ^2-157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-158]]

由無常故苦，非一切行。 ^2-158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-159]]

_何等空相？ ^2-159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-160]]

謂若於是處此非有，由此理正觀為空。 ^2-160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-161]]

若於是處餘是有，由此理如實知有， ^2-161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-162]]

是名善入空性。 如實知者， ^2-162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-163]]

不顛倒義。 ^2-163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-164]]

於何處誰非有？ ^2-164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-165]]

於蘊、界、處，常恒凝住不變壞法，我我所等非有。 ^2-165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-166]]

由此理，彼皆是空。 ^2-166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-167]]

於何處誰餘有？ ^2-167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-168]]

即此處無我性。 ^2-168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-169]]

此我無性，無我有性，是謂空性。 ^2-169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-170]]

故薄伽梵密意說言：有如實知有，無如實知無。 ^2-170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-171]]

復有三種空性， ^2-171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-172]]

謂自性空性、如性空性、真性空性。 ^2-172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-173]]

初依遍計所執自性觀， ^2-173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-174]]

第二依依他起自性觀， ^2-174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-175]]

第三依圓成實自性觀。 ^2-175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-176]]

_何等無我相？ ^2-176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-177]]

謂如我論者所立我相，蘊界處非此相， ^2-177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-178]]

由蘊界處我相無故，名無我相。 ^2-178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-179]]

故薄伽梵密意說言：一切法皆無我。 ^2-179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-180]]

如世尊說：此一切非我所，此非我 ^2-180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-181]]

處此非我我。 ^2-181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-182]]

於如是義，應以正慧如實觀察。 ^2-182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-183]]

此言何義？ ^2-183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-184]]

謂於外事密意說此一切非我所，於內事密意說此非我。處此非我我。 所以者何？ ^2-184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-185]]

以於外事唯計我所相，是故但遣我所； ^2-185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-186]]

於內事通計我我所相，是故雙遣我我所。 ^2-186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-187]]

_前說無常皆剎那相，此云何知？ ^2-187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-188]]

如心心所是剎那相，當知色等亦剎那相， ^2-188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-189]]

由心執受故、等心安危故、 ^2-189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-190]]

隨心轉變故、是心所依故、 ^2-190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-191]]

心增上生故、心自在轉故。 ^2-191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-192]]

又於最後位變壞可得故，生已不待緣自然滅壞故， ^2-192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-193]]

當觀色等亦念念滅。 ^2-193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-194]]

_如世尊說： ^2-194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-195]]

諸所有色， ^2-195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-196]]

彼一切若四大種、若四大種所造。 ^2-196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-197]]

此依何意說？ ^2-197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-198]]

依容有意說 ^2-198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-199]]

同在一處，依此而有是造義。 ^2-199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-200]]

若於此聚此大種可得，當知此聚唯有此大種非餘。 ^2-200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-201]]

或有聚唯一大種、 ^2-201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-202]]

或有二大種、 ^2-202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-203]]

或有乃至一切大種， ^2-203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-204]]

所造色亦爾。 若於此聚此所造色可得，當知此聚唯此非餘。 ^2-204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-205]]

或有聚唯一所造色、或二所造色、或有乃至多所造色，隨其所應。 ^2-205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-206]]

_又說麁聚色極微集所成者，當知此中極微無體， ^2-206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-207]]

但由覺慧漸漸分析細分損減，乃至可析邊際，即約此際建立極微。 ^2-207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-208]]

為遣一合想故，又為悟入諸所有色非真實故。 ^2-208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-209]]

_復次苦法略有八種差別，謂有廣大不寂靜苦、有寂靜苦、有寂靜不寂靜苦、 ^2-209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-210]]

有中不寂靜苦、 ^2-210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-211]]

有微薄不寂靜苦、 ^2-211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-212]]

有微薄寂靜苦、 ^2-212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-213]]

有極微薄寂靜苦、 ^2-213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-214]]

有非苦似苦住大寂靜。 ^2-214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-215]]

云何廣大不寂靜苦？ ^2-215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-216]]

謂生欲界，未曾積集諸善根者。 ^2-216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-217]]

云何寂靜苦？ ^2-217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-218]]

謂即此已生順解脫分善根者。 ^2-218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-219]]

云何寂靜不寂靜苦？ ^2-219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-220]]

謂即此為世間道離欲，已種善根者。 ^2-220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-221]]

云何中不寂靜苦？ ^2-221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-222]]

謂生色界，遠離順解脫分者。 ^2-222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-223]]

云何微薄不寂靜苦？ ^2-223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-224]]

謂生無色界，遠離順解脫分者。 ^2-224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-225]]

云何微薄寂靜苦？ ^2-225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-226]]

謂諸有學。 ^2-226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-227]]

云何極微薄寂靜苦？ ^2-227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-228]]

謂諸無學，命根住緣六處。 ^2-228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-229]]

云何非苦似苦住大寂靜？ ^2-229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-230]]

謂已得究竟菩薩摩訶薩等， ^2-230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-231]]

乘大悲願力故， ^2-231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-232]]

生諸有中。 ^2-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-233]]

復次前說死苦。 死有三種， ^2-233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-234]]

謂或善心死、或不善心死、或無記心死。 ^2-234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-235]]

善心死者，謂於明利心現行位，或由自善根力所持故、或由他所引攝故，發起善心趣命終位。 ^2-235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-236]]

不善心死者，謂亦於明利心現行位，或由自不善根力所持故、或由他所引攝故，起不善心趣命終位。 ^2-236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-237]]

無記心死者，謂若於明利心現行位、若於不明利心現行位、或由闕二緣故、或由加行無功能故，起無記心趣命終位。 ^2-237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-238]]

_修淨行者臨命終位，於身下分先起冷觸。 ^2-238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-239]]

不淨行者臨命終位，於身上分先起冷觸。 ^2-239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-240]]

不淨行者中有生時，其相顯現如黑羊羔光、或如陰暗夜分。 ^2-240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-241]]

修淨行者中有生時，其相顯現如白練光、或如晴夜分。 ^2-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-242]]

_又此中有，在欲色界正受生位， ^2-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-243]]

亦從無色界命終後位， ^2-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-244]]

亦名意生、健達縛等。 ^2-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-245]]

極住七日， ^2-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-246]]

或中夭、 ^2-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-247]]

或時移轉。 ^2-247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-248]]

_住中有中，亦能集諸業， ^2-248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-249]]

先串習力所引善等思現行故。 ^2-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-250]]

又能覩見同類有情。 ^2-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-251]]

又中有形似當生處。 ^2-251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-252]]

又此中有所趣無礙， ^2-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-253]]

如具神通往來迅速，仍於生處有所拘礙。 ^2-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-254]]

又此中有於所生處，如秤兩頭低昂道理，終沒結生時分亦爾。 ^2-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-255]]

住中有中，於所生處發起貪愛， ^2-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-256]]

亦用餘煩惱為緣助。 ^2-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-257]]

此中有身與貪俱滅，羯邏藍身與識俱生， ^2-257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-258]]

此唯是異熟。 ^2-258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-259]]

自此已後，根漸生長，如緣起中說。 ^2-259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-260]]

於四生類，或受卵生、或受胎生、或受濕生、或受化生。 ^2-260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-261]]

大乘阿毘達磨集論卷第四_無著菩薩造_三藏法師玄奘奉_詔譯_決擇分中諦品第一之二 ^2-261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-262]]

_云何集諦？ ^2-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-263]]

謂諸煩惱及煩惱增上所生諸業，俱說名集諦。 ^2-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-264]]

然薄伽梵隨最勝說，若愛若後有愛、若喜貪俱行愛、若彼彼喜樂愛，是名集諦。 ^2-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-265]]

言最勝者， ^2-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-266]]

是遍行義。 ^2-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-267]]

由愛具有六遍行義， ^2-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-268]]

是故最勝。 何等為六？ ^2-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-269]]

一事遍行、二位遍行、 ^2-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-270]]

三世遍行、四界遍行、 ^2-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-271]]

五求遍行、六種遍行。 ^2-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-272]]

_云何煩惱？ ^2-272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-273]]

謂由數故、相故、緣起故、境界故、相應故、差別故、邪行故、界故、眾故、斷故，觀諸煩惱。 ^2-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-274]]

_何等數故？ ^2-274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-275]]

謂或六或十六。 ^2-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-277]]

謂貪、瞋、慢、無明、疑、見， ^2-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-278]]

十謂前五見又分五， ^2-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-279]]

謂薩迦耶見、邊執見、邪見、見取、戒禁取。 ^2-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-280]]

_何等相故？ ^2-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-281]]

謂若法生時，相不寂靜， ^2-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-282]]

由此生故，身心相續不寂靜轉，是煩惱相。 ^2-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-283]]

_何等緣起故？ ^2-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-284]]

謂煩惱隨眠未永斷故、 ^2-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-285]]

順煩惱法現在前故、 ^2-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-286]]

不正思惟現前起故， ^2-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-287]]

如是煩惱方乃得生， ^2-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-288]]

是名緣起。 ^2-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-289]]

_何等境界故？ ^2-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-290]]

謂一切煩惱，還用一切煩惱為所緣境， ^2-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-291]]

及緣諸煩惱事。 ^2-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-292]]

又欲界煩惱，除無明見疑，餘不能緣上地為境。 ^2-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-293]]

上地諸煩惱不能緣下地為境， ^2-293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-294]]

已離彼地欲故。 ^2-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-295]]

又緣滅道諦諸煩惱，不能親緣滅道為境， ^2-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-296]]

唯由依彼妄起分別，說為所緣。 ^2-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-297]]

又煩惱有二種，謂緣無事及緣有事。 ^2-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-298]]

緣無事者，謂見及見相應法。 ^2-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-299]]

所餘煩惱，名緣有事。 ^2-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-300]]

_何等相應故？ ^2-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-301]]

謂貪不與瞋相應， ^2-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-302]]

如瞋疑亦爾， ^2-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-303]]

餘皆得相應。 ^2-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-304]]

如貪、瞋亦爾，謂瞋不與貪慢見相應， ^2-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-305]]

慢不與瞋、疑相應。 ^2-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-306]]

無明有二種：一一切煩惱相應無明、二不共無明。 ^2-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-307]]

不共無明者，謂於諦無智。 ^2-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-308]]

見不與瞋、疑相應。 ^2-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-309]]

疑不與貪、慢、見相應。 ^2-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-310]]

忿等隨煩惱更互不相應。 ^2-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-311]]

無慚、無愧於一切不善品中恒共相應。 ^2-311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-312]]

惛沈、掉舉、不信、懈怠、放逸，於一切染污品中恒共相應。 ^2-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-313]]

_何等差別故？ ^2-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-314]]

謂諸煩惱依種種義立種種門差別， ^2-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-315]]

所謂結、縛、隨眠、隨煩惱、 ^2-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-316]]

纏、暴流、軛、取、 ^2-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-317]]

繫、蓋、株杌、垢、 ^2-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-318]]

燒、 ^2-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-319]]

害、箭、所有、 ^2-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-320]]

惡行、漏、匱、熱、 ^2-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-321]]

惱、諍、熾然、稠林、拘礙等。 ^2-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-322]]

_結有幾種？ ^2-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-323]]

云何結？ 何處結耶？ ^2-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-324]]

結有九種， ^2-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-325]]

謂愛結、恚結、慢結、 ^2-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-326]]

無明結、見結、取結、 ^2-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-327]]

疑結、嫉結、慳結。 ^2-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-328]]

_愛結者，謂三界貪愛結所繫故 ^2-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-330]]

不厭三界， ^2-330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-331]]

由不厭故，廣行不善、不行諸善。 ^2-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-332]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-333]]

_恚結者，謂於有情苦及順苦法心有損害， ^2-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-334]]

恚結所繫故，於恚境相心不棄捨， ^2-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-335]]

不棄捨故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^2-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-336]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-337]]

_慢結者，即七慢， ^2-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-338]]

謂慢、過慢、慢過慢、我慢、 ^2-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-339]]

增上慢、下劣慢、邪慢。 ^2-339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-340]]

慢者， ^2-340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-341]]

謂於下劣計己為勝，或於不相似計己相似，心舉為性。 ^2-341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-342]]

過慢者， ^2-342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-343]]

謂於相似計己為勝，或復於勝計己相似，心舉為性。 ^2-343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-344]]

慢過慢者， ^2-344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-345]]

謂於勝己計己為勝，心舉為性。 ^2-345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-346]]

我慢者， ^2-346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-347]]

謂於五取蘊觀我我所，心舉為性。 ^2-347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-348]]

增上慢者， ^2-348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-349]]

謂於未得上勝證法，計己已得上勝證法，心舉為性。 ^2-349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-350]]

下劣慢者， ^2-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-351]]

謂於多分勝計己少分劣，心舉為性。 ^2-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-352]]

邪慢者， ^2-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-353]]

謂實無德計己有德，心舉為性。 ^2-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-354]]

慢結所繫故，於我我所不能了知， ^2-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-355]]

不了知故執我我所，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^2-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-356]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-357]]

_無明結者，謂三界無智，無明結所繫故， ^2-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-359]]

於苦法、集法不能解了， ^2-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-360]]

不解了故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^2-360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-361]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-362]]

_見結者，即三見， ^2-362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-363]]

謂薩迦耶見、邊執見、邪見。 ^2-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-364]]

見結所繫故，於邪出離，妄計追求。 ^2-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-365]]

妄興執著。 ^2-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-366]]

於邪出離，妄執著已，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^2-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-367]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-368]]

_取結者，謂見取，戒禁取。 ^2-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-369]]

取結所繫故，於邪出離方便妄計執著，以妄執著邪出離方便故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^2-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-370]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-371]]

_疑結者，謂於諦猶豫。 ^2-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-372]]

疑結所繫故，於佛、法、僧寶妄生疑惑。 ^2-372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-373]]

以疑惑故，於三寶所不修正行。 ^2-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-374]]

以於三寶所不修正行故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^2-374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-375]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-376]]

_嫉結者，謂耽著利養、不耐他榮發起心妬。 ^2-376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-377]]

嫉結所繫故，愛重利養、不尊敬法。 ^2-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-378]]

重利養故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^2-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-379]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-380]]

_慳結者，謂耽著利養，於資生具其心悋惜。 ^2-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-381]]

慳結所繫故，愛重畜積、不尊遠離。 ^2-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-382]]

重畜積故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^2-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-383]]

由此能招未來世苦，與苦相應。 ^2-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-384]]

_縛有三種， ^2-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-385]]

謂貪縛、瞋縛、癡縛。 ^2-385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-386]]

由貪縛故，縛諸有情令處壞苦。 ^2-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-387]]

由瞋縛故，縛諸有情令處苦苦。 ^2-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-388]]

由癡縛故，縛諸有情令處行苦。 ^2-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-389]]

又依貪、瞋、癡故， ^2-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-390]]

於善加行不得自在， ^2-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-391]]

故名為縛。 ^2-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-392]]

_隨眠有七， ^2-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-393]]

謂欲愛隨眠、瞋恚隨眠、有愛隨眠、 ^2-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-394]]

慢隨眠、無明隨眠、見隨眠、疑隨眠。 ^2-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-395]]

欲愛隨眠者，謂欲貪品麁重。 ^2-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-396]]

瞋恚隨眠者，謂瞋恚品麁重。 ^2-396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-397]]

有愛隨眠者，謂色無色貪品麁重。 ^2-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-398]]

慢隨眠者，謂慢品麁重。 ^2-398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-399]]

無明隨眠者，謂無明品麁重。 ^2-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-400]]

見隨眠者，謂見品麁重。 ^2-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-401]]

疑隨眠者，謂疑品麁重。 ^2-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-402]]

若未離欲求者，由欲愛瞋恚隨眠之所隨增。 ^2-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-403]]

未離有求者，由有愛隨眠之所隨增。 ^2-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-404]]

未離邪梵行求者，由慢、無明、見、疑隨眠之所隨增。 ^2-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-405]]

由彼眾生，得少對治便生憍慢。 ^2-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-406]]

愚於聖諦。 ^2-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-407]]

虛妄計度外邪解脫解脫方便。 ^2-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-408]]

於佛聖教正法毘柰耶中，猶豫疑惑。 ^2-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-409]]

_隨煩惱者，謂所有諸煩惱皆是隨煩惱。 ^2-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-410]]

有隨煩惱非煩惱， ^2-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-411]]

謂除煩惱所餘染污，行蘊所攝一切心所法。 ^2-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-412]]

此復云何？ 謂除貪等六煩惱所餘染污行蘊所攝忿等諸心所法。 ^2-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-413]]

又貪、瞋、癡，名隨煩惱心所法， ^2-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-414]]

由此隨煩惱，隨惱於心， ^2-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-415]]

令不離染、 ^2-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-416]]

令不解脫、 ^2-416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-417]]

令不斷障，故名隨煩惱。 ^2-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-418]]

如世尊說：汝等長夜，為貪、瞋、癡隨所惱亂，心恒染污。 ^2-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-419]]

_纏有八種，謂惛沈、睡眠、掉舉、惡作、 ^2-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-420]]

嫉、慳、無慚、無愧。 ^2-420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-421]]

謂隨修習止舉捨相，及彼所依梵行等所攝淨尸羅時，纏繞於心。 ^2-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-422]]

數數增盛纏繞於心，故名為纏。 ^2-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-423]]

_暴流有四，謂欲暴流、有暴流、見暴流、無明暴流。 ^2-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-424]]

隨流漂溺是暴流義， ^2-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-425]]

隨順雜染故。 ^2-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-426]]

初是習欲求者， ^2-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-427]]

第二是習有求者， ^2-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-428]]

後二是習邪梵行求者， ^2-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-429]]

能依、所依相應道理故。 ^2-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-430]]

_軛有四種， ^2-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-431]]

謂欲軛、有軛、見軛、無明軛。 ^2-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-432]]

障礙離繫是軛義，違背清淨故。 ^2-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-433]]

此亦隨其次第習三求者， ^2-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-434]]

相應現行。 ^2-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-435]]

_取有四種， ^2-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-436]]

謂欲取、見取、戒禁取、我語取。 ^2-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-437]]

執取諍根，執取後有，是取義。 ^2-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-438]]

所以者何？ ^2-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-439]]

由貪著欲，繫縛耽染為因，諸在家者更相鬪諍， ^2-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-440]]

此諍根本是第一取。 ^2-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-441]]

由貪著見，繫縛耽染為因，諸出家者更相鬪諍， ^2-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-442]]

此諍根本是後三取。 ^2-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-443]]

六十二見趣，是見取。 ^2-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-444]]

各別禁戒多分苦行，是戒禁取。 ^2-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-445]]

彼所依止薩迦耶見，是我語取。 ^2-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-446]]

由見取、戒禁取，諸外道輩更相諍論。 ^2-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-447]]

由我語取，諸外道輩互無諍論，與正法者互有諍論。 ^2-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-448]]

如是執著諍論根本，復能引取後有苦異熟， ^2-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-449]]

故名為取。 ^2-449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-450]]

_繫有四種， ^2-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-451]]

謂貪欲身繫、瞋恚身繫、戒禁取身繫。 此實執取身繫， ^2-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-452]]

以能障礙定意性身，故名為繫。 ^2-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-453]]

所以者何？ ^2-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-454]]

能為四種心亂因故。 ^2-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-455]]

謂由貪愛財物等因，令心散亂； ^2-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-456]]

於鬪諍事不正行為因，令心散亂； ^2-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-457]]

於難行戒禁苦惱為因，令心散亂； ^2-457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-458]]

不如正理推求境界為因，令心散亂。 ^2-458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-459]]

_蓋有五種， ^2-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-460]]

謂貪欲蓋、瞋恚蓋、惛沈睡眠蓋、掉舉惡作蓋、疑蓋。 ^2-460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-461]]

能令善品不得顯了，是蓋義， ^2-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-462]]

謂於樂出家位， ^2-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-463]]

覺邪行位， ^2-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-464]]

止、舉、捨位。 ^2-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-465]]

{能令善品不得顯了，是蓋義，} ^2-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-466]]

_株杌有三， ^2-466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-467]]

謂貪株杌、瞋株杌、癡株杌。 ^2-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-468]]

由依止貪、瞋、癡，先所串習為方便故，成貪等行， ^2-468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-469]]

心不調順，無所堪能難可解脫。 令諸有情難斷此行，故名株杌。 ^2-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-470]]

_垢有三種， ^2-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-471]]

謂貪垢、瞋垢、癡垢。 ^2-471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-472]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，毀犯如是尸羅學處。 ^2-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-473]]

由此有智同梵行者，或於聚落或閑靜處，見已作如是言： ^2-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-474]]

此長老作如是事、行如是行， ^2-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-475]]

為聚落刺，點染不淨。 ^2-475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-476]]

說名為垢。 ^2-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-477]]

_燒害有三， ^2-477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-478]]

謂貪燒害、瞋燒害、癡燒害。 ^2-478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-479]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，長時數受生死燒惱，故名燒害。 ^2-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-480]]

_箭有三種， ^2-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-481]]

謂貪箭、瞋箭、癡箭。 ^2-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-482]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，於有有具深起追求相續不絕， ^2-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-483]]

於佛、法、僧、苦、集、滅、道，常生疑惑，故名為箭。 ^2-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-484]]

_所有有三， ^2-484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-485]]

謂貪所有、瞋所有、癡所有。 ^2-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-486]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，積畜財物、有怖有怨、多住散亂，故名所有。 ^2-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-487]]

_惡行有三， ^2-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-488]]

謂貪惡行、瞋惡行、癡惡行。 ^2-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-489]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，恒行身、語、意惡行，故名惡行。 ^2-489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-490]]

又即依此貪、瞋、癡門，廣生無量惡不善行故，建立三不善根。 所以者何？ ^2-490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-491]]

以諸有情，愛味世間所有為因，行諸惡行；分別世間怨相為因，行諸惡行；執著世間邪法為因，行諸惡行， ^2-491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-492]]

是故此貪瞋癡亦名惡行、亦名不善根。 ^2-492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-493]]

_漏有三種， ^2-493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-494]]

謂欲漏、有漏、無明漏。 ^2-494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-495]]

令心連注流散不絕， ^2-495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-496]]

故名為漏。 此復云何？ ^2-496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-497]]

依外門流注，故立欲漏。 ^2-497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-498]]

依內門流注，故立有漏。 ^2-498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-499]]

依彼二所依門流注，故立無明漏。 ^2-499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-500]]

_匱有三種， ^2-500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-501]]

謂貪匱、瞋匱、癡匱。 ^2-501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-502]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，於有及資生具，恒起追求無有厭足，常為貧乏眾苦所惱，是故名匱。 ^2-502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-503]]

_熱有三種， ^2-503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-504]]

謂貪熱、瞋熱、癡熱。 ^2-504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-505]]

由依止貪、瞋、癡故，不如正理執著諸相、執著隨好。 ^2-505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-506]]

由執著相及隨好，故燒惱身心，故名為熱。 ^2-506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-507]]

_惱有三種， ^2-507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-508]]

謂貪惱、瞋惱、癡惱。 ^2-508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-509]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^2-509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-510]]

隨彼彼處愛樂耽著，彼若變壞便增愁歎， ^2-510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-511]]

種種憂苦熱惱所觸，故名為惱。 ^2-511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-512]]

_諍有三種， ^2-512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-513]]

謂貪諍、瞋諍、癡諍。 ^2-513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-514]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^2-514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-515]]

執持刀杖興諸戰諍種種鬪訟，是故貪等說名為諍。 ^2-515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-516]]

_熾然有三， ^2-516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-517]]

謂貪熾然、瞋熾然、癡熾然。 ^2-517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-518]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^2-518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-519]]

為非法貪大火所燒， ^2-519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-520]]

不平等貪大火所燒， ^2-520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-521]]

及為邪法大火所燒，故名熾然。 ^2-521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-522]]

_稠林有三， ^2-522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-523]]

謂貪稠林、瞋稠林、癡稠林。 ^2-523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-524]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^2-524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-525]]

於諸生死根本行中廣興染著， ^2-525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-526]]

令諸有情，感種種身流轉五趣，是故貪等說名稠林。 ^2-526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-527]]

_拘礙有三， ^2-527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-528]]

謂貪拘礙、瞋拘礙、癡拘礙。 ^2-528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-529]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^2-529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-530]]

顧戀身財無所覺了樂處憒閙，得少善法便生厭足。 ^2-530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-531]]

由此不能修諸善法，故名拘礙。 ^2-531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-532]]

_諸如是等煩惱義門，差別無量。 ^2-532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-533]]

_何等邪行故？ ^2-533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-534]]

謂貪瞋二煩惱，迷境界及見起邪行。 ^2-534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-535]]

慢，迷有情及見起邪行。 ^2-535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-536]]

薩迦耶見、邊執見、邪見，迷所知境起邪行。 ^2-536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-537]]

見取、戒禁取，迷諸見起邪行。 ^2-537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-538]]

疑，迷對治起邪行。 ^2-538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-539]]

無明，迷一切起邪行。 ^2-539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-540]]

又十煩惱，皆迷苦、集起諸邪行，是彼因緣所依處故。 ^2-540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-541]]

又十煩惱，皆迷滅、道起諸邪行，由此能生彼怖畏故。 ^2-541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-542]]

_何等界故？ ^2-542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-543]]

謂除瞋，餘一切通三界繫， ^2-543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-544]]

瞋唯欲界繫。 ^2-544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-545]]

_又貪，於欲界與樂、喜、捨相應。 ^2-545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-546]]

如於欲界，於初二靜慮亦爾， ^2-546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-547]]

於第三靜慮與樂、捨相應， ^2-547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-548]]

已上唯與捨相應。 ^2-548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-549]]

瞋與苦、憂、捨相應。 ^2-549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-550]]

慢，於欲界與喜、捨相應， ^2-550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-551]]

於初二靜慮與樂、喜、捨相應， ^2-551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-552]]

於第三靜慮與樂、捨相應， ^2-552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-553]]

已上唯捨相應。 ^2-553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-554]]

如慢，薩迦耶見、邊執見、見取、戒禁取亦爾。 ^2-554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-555]]

邪見，於欲界與憂、喜、捨相應， ^2-555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-556]]

於色、無色界，隨所有受皆與相應。 ^2-556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-557]]

疑，於欲界與憂、捨相應， ^2-557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-558]]

於色、無色界，隨所有受皆與相應。 ^2-558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-559]]

無明有二種，謂相應、不共。 ^2-559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-560]]

相應無明，一切煩惱相應故，若於是處，隨所有受皆得相應。 ^2-560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-561]]

不共無明，於欲界與憂、捨相應， ^2-561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-562]]

於上界隨所有受皆得相應。 ^2-562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-563]]

何故諸煩惱皆與捨相應？ ^2-563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-564]]

以一切煩惱墮中庸位，方息沒故。 ^2-564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-565]]

又貪，於欲界在六識身。 ^2-565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-566]]

如貪，瞋、無明亦爾。 ^2-566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-567]]

貪，於色界在四識身， ^2-567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-568]]

於無色界在意識身。 ^2-568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-569]]

如貪，無明亦爾。 ^2-569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-570]]

慢、見、疑，於一切處在意識身。 ^2-570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-571]]

又貪、瞋、慢，於欲界緣一分事轉； ^2-571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-572]]

如於欲界，於色無色界亦爾。 ^2-572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-573]]

所餘煩惱，於一切處遍緣一切事轉。 ^2-573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-574]]

_何等眾故？ ^2-574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-575]]

謂二眾煩惱：一見所斷眾、二修所斷眾。 ^2-575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-576]]

見所斷眾復有四種：一見苦所斷眾、二見集所斷眾、三見滅所斷眾、四見道所斷眾。 ^2-576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-577]]

欲界見苦所斷具十煩惱。 ^2-577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-578]]

如見苦所斷，見集滅道所斷亦爾。 ^2-578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-579]]

色界見苦等四種所斷各九煩惱，除瞋。 ^2-579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-581]]

如色界，無色界亦爾。 ^2-581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-582]]

如是見所斷煩惱眾，總有一百一十二煩惱。 ^2-582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-583]]

欲界修所斷有六煩惱， ^2-583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-584]]

謂俱生薩迦耶見、邊執見及貪、瞋、慢、無明。 ^2-584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-585]]

色界修所斷有五煩惱，除瞋。 ^2-585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-586]]

如色界，無色界亦爾。 ^2-586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-587]]

如是修所斷煩惱眾，總有十六煩惱。 ^2-587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-588]]

_何等斷故？ ^2-588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-589]]

謂如此差別斷、由此作意斷、從此而得斷。 ^2-589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-590]]

如此差別斷者， ^2-590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-591]]

謂遍智故、遠離故、得對治故。 ^2-591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-592]]

遍智者， ^2-592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-593]]

謂彼因緣事遍智、自體遍智、過患遍智。 ^2-593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-594]]

遠離者， ^2-594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-595]]

雖彼暫生而不堅執。 ^2-595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-596]]

得對治者， ^2-596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-597]]

謂未生者令不生故，已生者令斷故，得對治道。 ^2-597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-598]]

由此作意斷者，何等作意能斷耶？ ^2-598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-599]]

總緣作意，觀一切法皆無我性。 ^2-599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-600]]

能斷煩惱無常等行，但為修治無我行故。 ^2-600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-601]]

從此而得斷者，從何而得斷耶？ ^2-601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-602]]

不從過去，已滅故； ^2-602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-603]]

不從未來，未生故； ^2-603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-604]]

不從現在，道不俱故。 ^2-604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-605]]

然從諸煩惱麁重而得斷為斷。 ^2-605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-606]]

如是如是品麁重生，如是如是品對治。 ^2-606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-607]]

若此品對治生，即此品麁重滅。 平等平等， ^2-607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-608]]

猶如世間明生暗滅。 ^2-608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-609]]

由此品離繫故，令未來煩惱住不生法中，是名為斷。 ^2-609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-610]]

_云何煩惱增上所生諸業？ ^2-610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-611]]

謂若思業、若思已業， ^2-611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-612]]

總名業相。 ^2-612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-613]]

又有五種業： ^2-613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-614]]

一取受業、二作用業、三加行業、四轉變業、五證得業。 ^2-614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-615]]

今此義中，意多分別加行業。 ^2-615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-616]]

_何等思業？ ^2-616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-618]]

謂福業、非福業、不動業。 ^2-618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-619]]

_何等思已業？ ^2-619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-620]]

謂身業、語業、意業。 ^2-620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-621]]

_又此身、語、意三業，或善或不善。 ^2-621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-622]]

不善者，即十不善業道， ^2-622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-623]]

謂殺生、不與取、欲邪行、 ^2-623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-624]]

虛誑語、離間語、麁惡語、雜穢語、 ^2-624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-625]]

貪欲、瞋恚、邪見。 ^2-625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-626]]

善者即十善業道， ^2-626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-627]]

謂離殺生、離不與取、離欲邪行、離虛誑語、離離間語、離麁惡語、離雜穢語、 ^2-627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-628]]

無貪、無瞋、正見。 ^2-628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-629]]

_又殺生等，應以五門分別其相，謂事故、意樂故、方便故、煩惱故、究竟故。 ^2-629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-630]]

_如契經言：故思造業。 云何名為故思造業？ ^2-630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-631]]

謂他所教勅故思造業、他所勸請故思造業、無所了知故思造業、根本執著故思造業、顛倒分別故思造業。 ^2-631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-632]]

此中，根本執著故思造業、顛倒分別故思造業，若作若增長，非不受異熟。 ^2-632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-633]]

作者，謂起造諸業令其現行。 ^2-633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-634]]

增長者，謂令習氣增益。 ^2-634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-635]]

_如契經言：決定受業。 云何名為決定受業？ ^2-635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-636]]

謂作業決定、受異熟決定、分位決定。 ^2-636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-637]]

_十不善業道異熟果者，於三惡趣中。 ^2-637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-638]]

隨下中上品，受傍生、餓鬼、那落迦異熟。 ^2-638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-639]]

等流果者，各隨其相於人趣中，感得自身眾具衰損。 ^2-639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-640]]

增上果者，各隨其相，感得所有外事衰損。 ^2-640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-641]]

廣說如經：十善業道異熟果者，於人、天趣中受人天異熟。 ^2-641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-642]]

等流果者，即於彼處各隨其相，感得自身眾具興盛。 ^2-642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-643]]

增上果者，即於彼處各隨其相，感得所有外事興盛。 ^2-643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-644]]

_善不善業於善趣惡趣中感生異熟時，有招引業、圓滿業。 ^2-644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-645]]

招引業者，謂由此業能感異熟果。 ^2-645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-646]]

圓滿業者，謂由此業生已，領受愛不愛果。 ^2-646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-647]]

或有業由一業力牽得一身， ^2-647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-648]]

或有業由一業力牽得多身， ^2-648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-649]]

或有業由多業力牽得一身， ^2-649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-650]]

或有業由多業力牽得多身。 ^2-650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-651]]

若一有情成就多業，云何次第受異熟果？ ^2-651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-652]]

於彼身中，重者先熟； ^2-652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-653]]

或將死時，現在前者； ^2-653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-654]]

或先所數習者； ^2-654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-655]]

或最初所行者，彼異熟先熟。 ^2-655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-656]]

_如契經言：有三種業，謂福業、非福業、不動業。 ^2-656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-657]]

何等福業？ ^2-657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-658]]

謂欲界繫善業。 ^2-658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-659]]

何等非福業？ ^2-659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-660]]

謂不善業。 ^2-660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-661]]

何等不動業？ ^2-661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-662]]

謂色無色界繫善業。 ^2-662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-663]]

_如契經說：無明緣行，若福、非福及與不動。 ^2-663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-664]]

云何福及不動行緣無明生？ ^2-664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-665]]

有二種愚： ^2-665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-666]]

一異熟果愚、二真實義愚， ^2-666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-667]]

由異熟果愚，故發非福行。 ^2-667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-668]]

由真實義愚故，發福及不動行。 ^2-668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-669]]

_殺生業道，貪、瞋、癡為方便， ^2-669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-670]]

由瞋究竟。 ^2-670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-671]]

如殺生，麁惡語、瞋恚，業道亦爾。 ^2-671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-672]]

不與取業道，貪、瞋、癡為方便， ^2-672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-673]]

由貪究竟。 ^2-673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-674]]

如不與取，欲邪行、貪欲，業道亦爾。 ^2-674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-675]]

虛誑語業道，貪、瞋、癡為方便， ^2-675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-676]]

於三種中，隨由一究竟。 ^2-676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-677]]

如虛誑語，離間語、雜穢語業道亦爾。 ^2-677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-678]]

邪見業道，貪、瞋、癡為方便， ^2-678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-679]]

由癡究竟。 ^2-679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-680]]

_如契經言：有共業、有不共業，有強力業、有劣力業。 ^2-680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-681]]

云何共業？ ^2-681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-682]]

若業能令諸器世間種種差別。 ^2-682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-683]]

云何不共業？ ^2-683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-684]]

若業能令有情世間種種差別。 ^2-684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-685]]

或復有業令諸有情展轉增上，由此業力說諸有情更互相望為增上緣，以彼互有增上力故，亦名共業。 ^2-685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-686]]

是故經言：如是有情與餘有情互相見等，而不受用，不易可得。 ^2-686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-687]]

云何強力業？ ^2-687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-688]]

謂對治力強補特伽羅，故思所造諸不善業，由對治力所攝伏故， ^2-688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-689]]

令當受那落迦業，轉成現法受；應現法受業，轉令不受。 ^2-689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-690]]

所以此業名強力者，由能對治業力強故。 又故思所造一切善業皆名強力。 ^2-690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-691]]

依此業故， ^2-691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-692]]

薄伽梵說：我聖弟子能以無量廣大之業善熏其心， ^2-692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-693]]

諸所造作有量之業，不能牽引、不能留住，亦不能令墮在彼數。 ^2-693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-694]]

又對治力劣補特伽羅，故思所造諸不善業， ^2-694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-695]]

望諸善業皆名強力。 ^2-695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-696]]

又故思造業異熟，決定不斷不知，名強力業。 ^2-696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-697]]

此中意說， ^2-697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-698]]

一切善不善業異熟決定，聖道力不斷者，皆名強力業。 ^2-698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-699]]

又欲界繫諸不善業性皆是強力。 又先所串習名強力業。 ^2-699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-700]]

又依強位名強力業。又不可治者所造諸業名強力業，無涅槃法故。 ^2-700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-701]]

又由田故發強力業。又由心加行故發強力業。 ^2-701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-702]]

又由九種因發強力業， ^2-702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-703]]

謂由田故、事故、自體故、所依故、作意故、意樂故、助伴故、多修習故、與多眾生共所行故。 ^2-703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-704]]

與此相違是劣力業。 ^2-704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-705]]

如世尊說：若有說言， ^2-705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-706]]

彼彼丈夫補特伽羅，隨如是如是業，若作若增長，還受如是如是異熟。 ^2-706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-707]]

若有是事，便不應修清淨梵行，亦不可知正盡諸苦， ^2-707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-708]]

作苦邊際。 ^2-708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-709]]

若有說言，彼彼丈夫補特伽羅，隨如是如是順所受業， ^2-709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-710]]

若作若增長，還受如是如是順所受異熟。 ^2-710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-711]]

若有是事，便應修習清淨梵行，又亦可知正盡諸苦， ^2-711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-712]]

作苦邊際。 ^2-712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-713]]

如是經言，有何密意？ ^2-713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-714]]

此中佛意為欲遮止如是邪說，謂樂俱行業，還能感得樂俱行異熟。 ^2-714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-715]]

苦俱行業，還能感得苦俱行異熟；不苦不樂俱行業，還能感得不苦不樂俱行異熟，故作是說。 ^2-715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-716]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-717]]

順苦受者還受苦異熟，順不苦不樂受者還受不苦不樂異熟。 ^2-717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-718]]

苦俱行業，順樂受者還受樂異熟。 ^2-718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-719]]

順苦受者還受苦異熟，順不苦不樂受者還受不苦不樂異熟。 ^2-719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-720]]

不苦不樂俱行業，順樂受者還受樂異熟。 ^2-720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-721]]

順苦受者還受苦異熟，順不苦不樂受者還受不苦不樂異熟。 如是名為此經密意。 ^2-721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-722]]

又業差別有三種，謂律儀業、不律儀業、非律儀非不律儀業。 ^2-722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-724]]

謂別解脫律儀所攝業、靜慮律儀所攝業、無漏律儀所攝業。 ^2-724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-725]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-726]]

即是七眾所受律儀，謂苾芻律儀、苾芻尼律儀、式叉摩那律儀、勤策律儀、勤策女律儀、鄔波索迦律儀、鄔波斯迦律儀，及近住律儀。 ^2-726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-730]]

依能盡受遠離惡行、不遠離欲行補特伽羅。 ^2-730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-731]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-732]]

依止不能遠離惡行，及不能遠離欲行補特伽羅。 ^2-732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-733]]

若唯修學鄔波索迦一分學處， ^2-733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-734]]
