![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-735]]

應說成就而名犯戒。 ^2-735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-736]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-737]]

不遮彼受鄔波索迦律儀。 ^2-737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-738]]

然遮彼鄔波索迦性， ^2-738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-739]]

不堪親近承事苾芻苾芻尼等二出家眾故。 ^2-739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-740]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-741]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-743]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-745]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-746]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-747]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-748]]

是名無漏律儀所攝業。 ^2-748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-750]]

{} 何等名為不律儀者？ 所謂屠羊、養鷄養猪、捕鳥捕魚、獵鹿罝兔、劫賊、魁膾、控牛縛象、立壇呪龍、守獄、讒搆、好為損等。 ^2-750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-751]]

{謂諸不律儀者，或由生彼種姓中故、或由受持彼事業故，所期現行彼業決定。} ^2-751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-753]]

順樂受業者， ^2-755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-756]]

順苦受業者， ^2-757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-758]]

謂不善業。 ^2-758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-759]]

順不苦不樂受業者， ^2-759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-760]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-761]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-762]]

順現法受業者， ^2-762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-763]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-764]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-765]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-766]]

從預流果起、從阿羅漢果起亦爾。 ^2-766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-767]]

又於佛為上首僧中，造善惡業，必得現異熟。 ^2-767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-768]]

順生受業者， ^2-769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-770]]

復有所餘善不善業， ^2-771

順後受業者， ^2-773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-774]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-775]]

_又業差別有四種，謂黑黑異熟業、白白異熟業、黑白黑白異熟業、非黑白無異熟業能盡諸業。 ^2-775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-776]]

黑黑異熟業者， ^2-776

謂不善業。 ^2-777

白白異熟業者， ^2-778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-779]]

謂三界善業。 ^2-779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-780]]

黑白黑白異熟業者， ^2-780

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-781]]

謂欲界繫雜業， ^2-781

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-782]]

或有業意樂故黑、方便故白， ^2-782

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-783]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-784]]

非黑白無異熟業能盡諸業者， ^2-784

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-785]]

謂於加行無間道中諸無漏業。 ^2-785

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-786]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-787]]

_復有施等諸清淨業。 ^2-787

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-788]]

云何施業？ ^2-788

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-789]]

謂因緣故、等起故、處所故、自體故，分別施業。 ^2-789

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-790]]

因緣者，謂無貪、無瞋、無癡善根。 ^2-790

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-791]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-792]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-793]]

自體者，謂正行施時，身、語、意業。 ^2-793

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-795]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-796]]

又無所依施故、廣清淨施故、極歡喜施故、 ^2-796

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-797]]

數數施故、田器施故、善分布新舊施故，施得圓滿。 ^2-797

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-798]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-799]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-800]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-801]]

所施財物非穢離垢故、 ^2-801

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-802]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-803]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-804]]

如是應知施物圓滿。 ^2-804

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-805]]

_如契經說：成就尸羅，善能防護別解脫律儀，軌則所行皆悉圓滿，見微細罪生大怖畏，於諸學處善能受學。 ^2-805

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-806]]

云何成就尸羅？ ^2-806

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-807]]

能受能護淨尸羅故。 ^2-807

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-808]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-809]]

能善護持出離尸羅故。 ^2-809

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-810]]

云何軌則所行皆悉圓滿？ ^2-810

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-811]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-812]]

云何見微細罪生大怖畏？ ^2-812

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-813]]

勇猛恭敬所學尸羅故。 ^2-813

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-814]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-815]]

受學所學尸羅故。 ^2-815

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-816]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-818]]

由彼正解所攝持故。 ^2-818

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-819]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-820]]

終不毀犯所毀犯故。 ^2-820

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-821]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-822]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-824]]

染污尋思所不雜故。 ^2-824

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-825]]

云何身語無罪現行？ ^2-825

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-826]]

遠離邪願修梵行故。 ^2-826

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-827]]

云何身、語無害現行？ ^2-827

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-828]]

不輕陵他易共住故。 ^2-828

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-829]]

云何身、語隨順現行？ ^2-829

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-830]]

由能隨順涅槃得故。 ^2-830

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-831]]

云何身、語隨隱顯現行？ ^2-831

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-832]]

隱善顯惡故。 ^2-832

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-833]]

云何身、語親善現行？ ^2-833

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-834]]

同梵行者攝受尸羅故。 ^2-834

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-835]]

云何身、語應儀現行？ ^2-835

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-836]]

於尊尊位離憍慢故。 ^2-836

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-837]]

云何身、語敬順現行？ ^2-837

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-838]]

於尊教誨敬順受故。 ^2-838

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-839]]

云何身、語無熱現行？ ^2-839

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-840]]

遠離苦行熱惱下劣欲解故。 ^2-840

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-841]]

云何身、語不惱現行？ ^2-841

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-842]]

棄捨財業無悔惱故。 ^2-842

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-843]]

云何身、語無悔現行？ ^2-843

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-844]]

雖得少分不以為喜，而無悔恨故。 ^2-844

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-845]]

如世尊說：如是有情皆由自業，業所乖諍、從業所生、依業出離， ^2-845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-846]]

業能分別一切有情高下勝劣。 ^2-846

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-847]]

云何有情皆由自業？ ^2-847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-848]]

由自造業而受異熟故。 ^2-848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-849]]

云何業所乖諍？ ^2-849

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-850]]

於受自業所得異熟時，善不善業互違諍故。 ^2-850

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-851]]

云何從業所生？ ^2-851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-852]]

是諸有情遠離無因惡因，唯從業所生故。 ^2-852

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-853]]

云何依業出離？ ^2-853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-854]]

依對治業，解業縛故。 ^2-854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-855]]

云何由業有情高下？ ^2-855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-856]]

謂猶業故於善惡趣得自體差別。 ^2-856

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-857]]

云何勝劣？ ^2-857

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-858]]

謂諸有情成就功德過失差別。 ^2-858

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-859]]

_如世尊說：有情業異熟不可思議。 ^2-859

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-860]]

云何業異熟可思議？ 云何業異熟不可思議？ ^2-860

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-861]]

謂諸善業於人天趣，得可愛異熟，是可思議。 ^2-861

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-862]]

諸不善業墮三惡趣，得不愛異熟，是可思議。 ^2-862

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-863]]

即由此業，感諸有情自身異熟等種種差別，不可思議。 ^2-863

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-864]]

又即善不善業，處差別、事差別、因差別、異熟差別、品類差別等，皆不可思議。 ^2-864

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-865]]

復有種種外事差別，能感業用不可思議。 ^2-865

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-866]]

又末尼珠、藥草、呪術，相應業用不可思議。 ^2-866

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-867]]

又諸觀行者，威德業用不可思議。 ^2-867

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-868]]

又諸菩薩，自在業用不可思議， ^2-868

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-869]]

所謂命自在故、心自在故、財自在故、業自在故、 ^2-869

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-870]]

生自在故、勝解自在故、願自在故、神通自在故、 ^2-870

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-871]]

智自在故、法自在故。 ^2-871

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-872]]

諸大菩薩由如是等自在力故，所作業用不可思議。 ^2-872

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-873]]

又一切佛，所作諸佛應所作事業用不可思議。 ^2-873

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-874]]

如是集諦總有四種行相差別， ^2-874

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-875]]

謂因相、集相、生相、緣相。 ^2-875

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-876]]

云何因相？ ^2-876

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-877]]

謂能引發復有習氣因，是名因相。 ^2-877

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-878]]

云何集相？ ^2-878

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-879]]

謂彼彼有情所集習氣，於彼彼有情類為等起因，是名集相。 ^2-879

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-880]]

云何生相？ ^2-880

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-881]]

謂各別內身無量品類差別生因，是名生相。 ^2-881

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-882]]

云何緣相？ ^2-882

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-883]]

謂諸有情別別得捨因，是名緣相。 ^2-883

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-884]]

_大乘阿毘達磨集論卷第四 ^2-884

## 大乘阿毘達磨集論 — 3/4 ^3-0

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1]]

大乘阿毘達磨集論卷第五_無著菩薩造_三藏法師玄奘奉_詔譯_決擇分中諦品第一之三_ ^3-1

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-3]]

云何滅諦？ ^3-3

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-4]]

謂相故、 ^3-4

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-5]]

甚深故、 ^3-5

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-6]]

世俗故、 ^3-6

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-7]]

勝義故、 ^3-7

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-8]]

不圓滿故、 ^3-8

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-9]]

圓滿故、 ^3-9

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-10]]

無莊嚴故、 ^3-10

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-11]]

有莊嚴故、 ^3-11

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-12]]

有餘故、 ^3-12

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-13]]

無餘故、 ^3-13

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-14]]

最勝故、 ^3-14

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-15]]

差別故，分別滅諦。 ^3-15

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-16]]

_何等相故？ ^3-16

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-17]]

謂真如、聖道、煩惱不生， ^3-17

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-18]]

若滅依、若能滅、若滅性，是滅諦相。 ^3-18

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-19]]

如世尊說： ^3-19

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-20]]

眼、耳及與鼻、舌、身及與意， ^3-20

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-21]]

於此處名色，究竟滅無餘。 ^3-21

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-22]]

又說： ^3-22

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-23]]

是故汝今當觀是處， ^3-23

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-24]]

所謂此處眼究竟滅， ^3-24

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-25]]

遠離色想， ^3-25

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-26]]

乃至意究竟滅， ^3-26

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-27]]

遠離法想。 ^3-27

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-28]]

由此道理顯示所緣真如境上，有漏法滅，是滅諦相。 ^3-28

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-29]]

_何等甚深故？ ^3-29

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-30]]

謂彼諸行究竟寂滅，如是寂滅望彼諸行，不可說異、 ^3-30

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-31]]

不可說不異、 ^3-31

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-32]]

不可說亦異亦不異、 ^3-32

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-33]]

不可說非異非不異。 ^3-33

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-34]]

所以者何？ ^3-34

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-35]]

無戲論故。 ^3-35

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-36]]

於此義中若生戲論，非正思議、 ^3-36

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-37]]

非道、非如，亦非善巧方便思故。 ^3-37

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-38]]

如世尊說：此六觸處盡、 ^3-38

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-39]]

離欲、 ^3-39

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-40]]

滅寂、 ^3-40

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-41]]

靜、 ^3-41

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-42]]

沒等。 ^3-42

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-44]]

若謂有異、 ^3-44

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-45]]

若謂無異、 ^3-45

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-46]]

若謂亦有異亦無異、 ^3-46

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-47]]

若謂非有異非無異者， ^3-47

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-48]]

於無戲論便生戲論。 ^3-48

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-49]]

乃至有六處可有諸戲論。 ^3-49

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-50]]

六處既滅絕諸戲論， ^3-50

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-51]]

即是涅槃。 ^3-51

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-52]]

_何等世俗故？ ^3-52

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-53]]

謂以世間道摧伏種子所得滅， ^3-53

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-54]]

是故，世尊別名說為彼分涅槃。 ^3-54

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-55]]

_何等勝義故？ ^3-55

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-56]]

謂以聖慧，永拔種子所得滅。 ^3-56

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-57]]

_何等不圓滿故？ ^3-57

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-58]]

謂諸有學或預流果攝、 ^3-58

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-59]]

或一來果攝、 ^3-59

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-60]]

或不還果攝， ^3-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-61]]

等所有滅。 ^3-61

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-62]]

_何等圓滿故？ ^3-62

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-63]]

謂諸無學阿羅漢果攝等所有滅。 ^3-63

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-64]]

_何等無莊嚴故？ ^3-64

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-65]]

謂慧解脫阿羅漢所有滅。 ^3-65

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-66]]

_何等有莊嚴故？ ^3-66

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-67]]

謂俱分解脫，三明六通阿羅漢等所有滅。 ^3-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-68]]

_何等有餘故？ ^3-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-69]]

謂有餘依滅。 ^3-69

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-70]]

_何等無餘故？ ^3-70

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-71]]

謂無餘依滅。 ^3-71

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-72]]

_何等最勝故？ ^3-72

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-73]]

謂佛菩薩無住涅槃攝所有滅， ^3-73

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-74]]

以常安住一切有情利樂事故。 ^3-74

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-75]]

_何等差別故？ ^3-75

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-76]]

謂無餘、 ^3-76

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-77]]

永斷、 ^3-77

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-78]]

永出、 ^3-78

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-79]]

永吐盡、 ^3-79

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-80]]

離欲、 ^3-80

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-81]]

滅、 ^3-81

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-82]]

寂靜、 ^3-82

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-83]]

沒 ^3-83

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-84]]

等。 ^3-84

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-85]]

何故名無餘永斷？ ^3-85

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-86]]

由餘句故。 ^3-86

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-87]]

何故名永出？ ^3-87

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-88]]

永出諸纏故。 ^3-88

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-89]]

何故名永吐？ ^3-89

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-90]]

永吐隨眠故。 ^3-90

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-91]]

何故名盡？ ^3-91

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-92]]

見道對治得離繫故。 ^3-92

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-93]]

何故名離欲？ ^3-93

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-94]]

修道對治得離繫故。 ^3-94

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-95]]

何故名滅？ ^3-95

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-96]]

當來彼果苦不生故。 ^3-96

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-97]]

何故名寂靜？ ^3-97

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-98]]

於現法中，彼果心苦永不行故。 ^3-98

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-99]]

何故名沒？ ^3-99

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-100]]

餘所有事永滅沒故。 ^3-100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-101]]

何故此滅，復名無為？ ^3-101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-102]]

離三相故。 ^3-102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-103]]

何故此滅，復名難見？ ^3-103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-104]]

超過肉眼、天眼境故。 ^3-104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-105]]

何故此滅，復名不轉？ ^3-105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-106]]

永離諸趣差別轉故。 ^3-106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-107]]

何故此滅，名不卑屈？ ^3-107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-108]]

離三愛故。 ^3-108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-109]]

何故此滅，復名甘露？ ^3-109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-111]]

離蘊魔故。 ^3-111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-112]]

何故此滅，復名無漏？ ^3-112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-113]]

永離一切煩惱魔故。 ^3-113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-114]]

何故此滅，復名舍宅？ ^3-114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-115]]

無罪喜樂所依事故。 ^3-115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-116]]

何故此滅，復名洲渚？ ^3-116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-117]]

三界隔絕故。 ^3-117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-118]]

何故此滅，復名弘濟？ ^3-118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-119]]

能遮一切大苦災橫故。 ^3-119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-120]]

何故此滅，復名歸依？ ^3-120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-121]]

無有虛妄意樂方便所依處故。 ^3-121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-122]]

何故此滅，名勝歸趣？ ^3-122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-123]]

能為歸趣一切最勝聖性所依處故。 ^3-123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-124]]

何故此滅，復名不死？ ^3-124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-125]]

永離生故。 ^3-125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-126]]

何故此滅，名無熱惱？ ^3-126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-127]]

永離一切煩惱熱故、永離一切求不得苦大熱惱故。 ^3-127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-128]]

何故此滅，名無熾然？ ^3-128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-129]]

永離一切愁、歎、憂、苦諸惱亂故。 ^3-129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-130]]

何故此滅，復名安隱？ ^3-130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-131]]

離怖畏住所依處故。 ^3-131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-132]]

何故此滅，復名清涼？ ^3-132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-133]]

諸利益事所依處故。 ^3-133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-134]]

何故此滅，復名樂事？ ^3-134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-135]]

第一義樂事故。 ^3-135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-136]]

何故此滅，名趣吉祥？ ^3-136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-137]]

為證得彼易修方便所依處故。 ^3-137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-138]]

何故此滅，復名無病？ ^3-138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-139]]

永離一切障礙病故。 ^3-139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-140]]

何故此滅，復名不動？ ^3-140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-141]]

永離一切散動故。 ^3-141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-142]]

何故此滅，復名涅槃？ ^3-142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-143]]

無相寂滅大安樂住所依處故。 ^3-143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-144]]

何故此滅，復名無生？ ^3-144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-145]]

離續生故。 ^3-145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-146]]

何故此滅，復名無起？ ^3-146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-147]]

永離此後漸生起故。 ^3-147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-148]]

何故此滅，復名無造？ ^3-148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-149]]

永離前際諸業煩惱勢力所引故。 ^3-149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-150]]

何故此滅，復名無作？ ^3-150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-151]]

不作現在諸業煩惱所依處故。 ^3-151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-152]]

何故此滅，復名不生？ ^3-152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-153]]

永離未來相續生故。 ^3-153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-154]]

_如是滅諦總有四種行相差別， ^3-154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-155]]

謂滅相、 ^3-155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-156]]

靜相、 ^3-156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-157]]

妙相、 ^3-157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-158]]

離相。 ^3-158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-159]]

云何滅相？ ^3-159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-160]]

煩惱離繫故。 ^3-160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-161]]

云何靜相？ ^3-161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-162]]

苦離繫故。 ^3-162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-163]]

云何妙相？ ^3-163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-164]]

樂靜事故。 ^3-164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-165]]

云何離相？ ^3-165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-166]]

常利益事故。 ^3-166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-167]]

_云何道諦？ ^3-167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-168]]

謂由此道故知苦、 ^3-168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-169]]

斷集、 ^3-169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-170]]

證滅、 ^3-170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-171]]

修道， ^3-171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-172]]

是略說道諦相。 ^3-172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-173]]

道有五種， ^3-173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-174]]

謂資糧道、 ^3-174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-175]]

加行道、 ^3-175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-176]]

見道、 ^3-176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-177]]

修道、 ^3-177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-178]]

究竟道。 ^3-178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-179]]

_何等資糧道？ ^3-179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-180]]

謂諸異生所有尸羅， ^3-180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-181]]

守護根門， ^3-181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-182]]

飲食知量， ^3-182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-183]]

初夜、後夜常不睡眠，勤修止觀， ^3-183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-184]]

正知而住。 ^3-184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-185]]

復有所餘，進習諸善， ^3-185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-186]]

聞所成慧、 ^3-186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-187]]

思所成慧、 ^3-187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-188]]

修所成慧， ^3-188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-189]]

修習此故，得成現觀解脫所依器性。 ^3-189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-190]]

_何等加行道？ ^3-190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-191]]

謂有資糧道，皆是加行道， ^3-191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-192]]

或有加行道，非資糧道。 ^3-192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-193]]

謂已積集資糧道者，所有順決擇分善根， ^3-193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-194]]

謂煖法、 ^3-194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-195]]

頂法、 ^3-195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-196]]

順諦忍法、 ^3-196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-197]]

世第一法。 ^3-197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-198]]

云何煖法？ ^3-198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-199]]

謂各別內證，於諸諦中明得三摩地， ^3-199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-200]]

鉢羅若及彼相應等法。 ^3-200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-201]]

云何頂法？ ^3-201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-202]]

謂各別內證，於諸諦中明增三摩地， ^3-202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-203]]

鉢羅若及彼相應等法。 ^3-203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-204]]

云何順諦忍法？ ^3-204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-205]]

謂各別內證，於諸諦中一分已入隨順三摩地，鉢羅若及彼相應等法。 ^3-205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-206]]

云何世第一法？ ^3-206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-207]]

謂各別內證，於諸諦中無間心三摩地，鉢羅若及彼相應等法。 ^3-207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-208]]

何等見道？ ^3-208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-209]]

若總說，謂世第一法無間無所得三摩地，鉢羅若及彼相應等法。 ^3-209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-210]]

又所緣、能緣平等，平等智為其相。 ^3-210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-211]]

又遣各別有情假、 ^3-211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-212]]

法假， ^3-212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-213]]

遍遣二假所緣法智為相。 ^3-213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-214]]

_若別說見道差別， ^3-214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-215]]

謂世第一法無間，苦法智忍、 ^3-215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-216]]

苦法智、 ^3-216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-217]]

苦類智忍、 ^3-217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-218]]

苦類智， ^3-218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-219]]

集法智忍、 ^3-219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-220]]

集法智、 ^3-220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-221]]

集類智忍、 ^3-221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-222]]

集類智， ^3-222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-223]]

滅法智忍、 ^3-223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-224]]

滅法智、 ^3-224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-225]]

滅類智忍、 ^3-225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-226]]

滅類智， ^3-226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-227]]

道法智忍、 ^3-227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-228]]

道法智、 ^3-228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-229]]

道類智忍、 ^3-229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-230]]

道類智。 ^3-230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-231]]

如是十六智忍，是見道差別相。 ^3-231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-232]]

_云何苦？ ^3-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-233]]

謂苦諦。 ^3-233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-234]]

云何苦法？ ^3-234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-235]]

謂苦諦增上所起教法。 ^3-235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-236]]

云何法智？ ^3-236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-237]]

謂於加行道中，觀察諦增上法智。 ^3-237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-238]]

云何智忍？ ^3-238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-239]]

謂先觀察增上力故， ^3-239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-240]]

於各別苦諦中，起現證無漏慧， ^3-240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-241]]

由此慧故，永捨見苦所斷一切煩惱， ^3-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-242]]

是故名為苦法智忍。 ^3-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-243]]

云何苦法智？ ^3-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-244]]

謂忍無間，由此智故，於前所說煩惱解脫而得作證， ^3-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-245]]

是名苦法智。 ^3-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-246]]

云何苦類智忍？ ^3-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-247]]

謂苦法智無間，無漏慧生，於苦法智忍， ^3-247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-248]]

及苦法智各別內證。 言後諸聖法皆是此種類，是故名為苦類智忍。 ^3-248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-249]]

云何苦類智？ ^3-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-250]]

謂此無間無漏智生，審定印可苦類智忍， ^3-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-251]]

是名苦類智。 ^3-251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-252]]

如是於餘諦中，隨其所應，諸忍、諸智盡當知。 ^3-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-253]]

_於此位中，由法忍、法智覺悟所取、 ^3-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-254]]

由類忍、類智覺悟能取。 ^3-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-255]]

又此一切忍智位中，說名安住無相觀者。 ^3-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-256]]

如是十六心剎那，說名見道。 ^3-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-257]]

於所知境智生究竟，名一剎那。 ^3-257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-258]]

_一切道諦由四種相應隨覺了，謂安立故、思惟故、證受故、圓滿故。 ^3-258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-259]]

云何安立故？ ^3-259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-260]]

謂聲聞等隨自所證已得究竟，為欲令他亦了知故，由後得智，以無量種名句文身，安立道諦。 ^3-260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-261]]

云何思惟故？ ^3-261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-262]]

謂正修習現觀方便，以世間智，如所安立思惟數習。 ^3-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-263]]

云何證受故？ ^3-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-264]]

謂如是數習已，自內證受最初見道，正出世間無戲論位。 ^3-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-265]]

云何圓滿故？ ^3-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-266]]

謂此位後圓滿轉依，乃至證得究竟。 ^3-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-267]]

彼既證得究竟位已，復由後得智，以名句文身安立道諦。 ^3-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-268]]

_如契經言，遠塵離垢於諸法中正法眼生者，此依見道說， ^3-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-269]]

諸法忍能遠塵， ^3-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-270]]

諸法智能離垢。 ^3-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-271]]

遍知故、永斷故，道得清淨。 ^3-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-272]]

_如契經言： ^3-272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-273]]

見法、 ^3-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-274]]

得法、 ^3-274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-275]]

極通達法、 ^3-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-276]]

究竟堅法， ^3-276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-277]]

越度一切悕望、疑惑， ^3-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-278]]

不假他緣。 ^3-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-279]]

於大師教餘不能引， ^3-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-280]]

於諸法中得無所畏，此亦依見道說。 ^3-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-281]]

見法者，謂諸法忍。 ^3-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-282]]

得法者，謂諸法智。 ^3-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-283]]

極通達法者，謂諸類忍。 ^3-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-284]]

究竟堅法者，謂諸類智。 ^3-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-285]]

越度一切悕望者，由諸忍智，於自所證無有悕慮。 ^3-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-286]]

越度一切疑惑者，於此位中，於他所證無有猶豫。 ^3-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-287]]

不假他緣者，於所修道中，無他引道自然善巧。 ^3-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-288]]

於大師教餘不能引者，於佛聖教不為邪道所化引故。 ^3-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-289]]

於諸法中得無所畏者，於依所證問記法中， ^3-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-290]]

諸怯劣心永無有故。 ^3-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-291]]

_何等修道？ ^3-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-292]]

謂見道上所有世間道、 ^3-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-293]]

出世間道、 ^3-293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-294]]

軟道、 ^3-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-295]]

中道、 ^3-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-296]]

上道、 ^3-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-297]]

加行道、 ^3-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-298]]

無間道、 ^3-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-299]]

解脫道、 ^3-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-300]]

勝進道等，皆名修道。 ^3-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-301]]

_云何世間道？ ^3-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-302]]

謂世間初靜慮、 ^3-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-303]]

第二靜慮、 ^3-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-304]]

第三靜慮、 ^3-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-305]]

第四靜慮， ^3-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-306]]

空無邊處、 ^3-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-307]]

識無邊處、 ^3-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-308]]

無所有處、 ^3-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-309]]

非想非非想處。 ^3-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-310]]

如是靜慮、無色，由四種相應廣分別，謂雜染故、 ^3-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-311]]

清白故、 ^3-311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-312]]

建立故、 ^3-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-313]]

清淨故。 ^3-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-314]]

何等雜染故？ ^3-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-315]]

謂四無記根：一愛、二見、三慢、四無明。 ^3-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-316]]

由有愛故，味上靜慮雜染所染， ^3-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-317]]

由有見故，見上靜慮雜染所染， ^3-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-318]]

由有慢故，慢上靜慮雜染所染， ^3-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-319]]

由無明故，疑上靜慮雜染所染。 ^3-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-320]]

如是煩惱恒染其心，令色、無色界煩惱、隨煩惱相續流轉。 ^3-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-321]]

_何等清白故？ ^3-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-322]]

謂淨靜慮、無色，由性善故，說名清白。 ^3-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-323]]

_何等建立故？ ^3-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-324]]

有四種建立， ^3-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-325]]

謂支分建立、 ^3-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-326]]

等至建立、 ^3-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-327]]

品類建立、 ^3-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-328]]

名想建立。 ^3-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-329]]

_云何支分建立？ ^3-329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-330]]

謂初靜慮有五支。 ^3-330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-331]]

何等為五？ ^3-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-332]]

一尋、二伺、三喜、四樂、五心一境性。 ^3-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-333]]

第二靜慮有四支。 ^3-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-334]]

何等為四？ ^3-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-335]]

一內等淨、二喜、三樂、四心一境性。 ^3-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-336]]

第三靜慮有五支。 ^3-336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-337]]

何等為五？ ^3-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-338]]

一捨、二念、三正知、四樂、五心一境性。 ^3-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-339]]

第四靜慮有四支。 ^3-339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-340]]

何等為四？ ^3-340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-341]]

一捨清淨、 ^3-341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-342]]

二念清淨、 ^3-342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-343]]

三不苦不樂受、 ^3-343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-344]]

四心一境性。 ^3-344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-345]]

對治支故，利益支故，彼二所依自性支故， ^3-345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-346]]

諸無色中不立支分，以奢摩他一味性故。 ^3-346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-347]]

云何等至建立？ ^3-347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-348]]

謂由七種作意，證入初靜慮， ^3-348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-349]]

如是乃至非想非非想處。 ^3-349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-350]]

何等名為七種作意？ ^3-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-351]]

謂了相作意、 ^3-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-352]]

勝解作意、 ^3-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-353]]

遠離作意、 ^3-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-354]]

攝樂作意、 ^3-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-355]]

觀察作意、 ^3-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-356]]

加行究竟作意、 ^3-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-357]]

加行究竟果作意。 ^3-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-358]]

_云何品類建立？ ^3-358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-359]]

謂初靜慮具軟、中、上三品熏修。 ^3-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-360]]

如初靜慮，餘靜慮及無色三品熏修亦爾。 ^3-360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-361]]

由軟、中、上品熏修初靜慮故，於初靜慮中還生三異熟。 ^3-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-362]]

如初靜慮，於餘靜慮中，若熏修若生果各三品亦爾。 ^3-362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-363]]

於無色界中無別處所故，不立生果處所差別。 ^3-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-364]]

然由三品熏修無色定故，彼異熟生時有高有下、有劣有勝。 ^3-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-365]]

_云何名想建立？ ^3-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-366]]

謂於初靜慮所攝定中， ^3-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-367]]

諸佛世尊及得究竟大威德菩薩摩訶薩所入三摩地，彼三摩地一切聲聞及獨覺等，尚不了其名， ^3-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-368]]

豈能知數？ 況復證入？ ^3-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-369]]

如於初靜慮所攝定中，於餘靜慮無色所攝定中亦爾。 ^3-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-370]]

如是所說皆依靜慮波羅蜜多。 ^3-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-371]]

_何等清淨故？ ^3-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-372]]

謂初靜慮中邊際定，乃至非想非非想處邊際定，是名清淨。 ^3-372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-373]]

云何出世道？ ^3-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-374]]

謂於修道中 ^3-374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-375]]

苦智、集智、滅智、道智， ^3-375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-376]]

法智類智品所攝 ^3-376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-377]]

及彼相應三摩地等， ^3-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-378]]

或未至定所攝、 ^3-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-379]]

或初靜慮乃至無所有處所攝， ^3-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-380]]

非想非非想處，唯是世間不明了想恒現行故， ^3-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-381]]

由此道理故名無想。 ^3-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-382]]

如世尊言： ^3-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-383]]

乃至有想三摩鉢底， 方能如實照了通達。 ^3-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-384]]

滅定亦是出世間攝， ^3-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-386]]

由聖道後所證得故。 ^3-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-387]]

要於人趣方能引發， ^3-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-388]]

或於人趣、或於色界能現在前， ^3-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-389]]

生無色界多不現起， ^3-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-390]]

由住寂靜解脫異熟者，於此滅定多不發起勤方便故。 ^3-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-391]]

_云何軟道？ ^3-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-392]]

謂軟軟、軟中、軟上品道。 ^3-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-393]]

由此道故，能捨三界所繫地、地中，上上、上中、上下三品煩惱。 ^3-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-394]]

_云何中道？ ^3-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-395]]

謂中軟、中中、中上品道。 ^3-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-396]]

由此道故，能捨三界所繫地、地中，中上、中中、中軟三品煩惱。 ^3-396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-397]]

_云何上道？ ^3-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-398]]

謂上軟、上中、上上品道。 ^3-398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-399]]

由此道故，能捨三界所繫地、地中，軟上、軟中、軟軟三品煩惱。 ^3-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-400]]

_云何加行道？ ^3-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-401]]

謂由此道能捨煩惱， ^3-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-402]]

是名修道中加行道。 ^3-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-403]]

_云何無間道？ ^3-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-404]]

謂由此道，無間永斷煩惱令無所餘。 ^3-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-405]]

_云何解脫道？ ^3-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-406]]

謂由此道證斷煩惱所得解脫。 ^3-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-407]]

云何勝進道？ ^3-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-408]]

謂為斷餘品煩惱所有加行、無間、解脫道，是名勝進道。 ^3-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-409]]

又復棄捨斷煩惱加行，或勤方便，思惟諸法， ^3-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-410]]

或勤方便安住諸法， ^3-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-411]]

或進修餘三摩鉢底諸所有道， ^3-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-412]]

名勝進道。 ^3-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-413]]

又為引發勝品功德，或復安住諸所有道， ^3-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-414]]

名勝進道。 ^3-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-415]]

_復云何修如是諸道？ ^3-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-416]]

謂得修、 ^3-416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-417]]

習修、 ^3-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-418]]

除去修、 ^3-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-419]]

對治修。 ^3-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-420]]

得修者， ^3-420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-421]]

謂未生善法，修習令生。 ^3-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-422]]

習修者， ^3-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-423]]

謂已生善法修令堅住不忘，倍復增廣。 ^3-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-424]]

除去修者， ^3-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-425]]

謂已生惡不善法，修令永斷。 ^3-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-426]]

對治修者， ^3-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-427]]

謂未生惡不善法，修令不生。 ^3-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-428]]

又道生時，能安立自習氣，是名得修。 ^3-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-429]]

即此道現前修習，是名習修。 ^3-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-430]]

即此道現在前時，能捨自障，名除去修。 ^3-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-431]]

即此道既捨自障，令彼未來住不生法，名對治修。 ^3-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-432]]

復有四種對治名對治修， ^3-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-433]]

謂厭壞對治、 ^3-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-434]]

斷對治、 ^3-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-435]]

持對治、 ^3-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-436]]

遠分對治。 ^3-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-437]]

云何厭壞對治？ ^3-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-438]]

謂於有漏諸行見多過患。 ^3-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-439]]

云何斷對治？ ^3-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-440]]

謂加行道及無間道。 ^3-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-441]]

云何持對治？ ^3-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-442]]

謂解脫道。 ^3-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-443]]

云何遠分對治？ ^3-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-444]]

謂此後諸道。 ^3-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-445]]

_又道差別有十一種， ^3-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-446]]

謂觀察事道、 ^3-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-447]]

勤功用道、 ^3-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-448]]

修治定道、 ^3-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-449]]

現觀方便道、 ^3-449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-450]]

親近現觀道、 ^3-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-451]]

現觀道、 ^3-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-452]]

清淨出離道、 ^3-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-453]]

依根差別道、 ^3-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-454]]

淨修三學道、 ^3-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-455]]

發諸功德道、 ^3-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-456]]

遍攝諸道道。 ^3-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-457]]

謂三十七菩提分法、 ^3-457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-459]]

四種正行、 ^3-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-460]]

四種法迹、 ^3-460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-461]]

奢摩他、 ^3-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-462]]

毘鉢舍那、 ^3-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-463]]

三無漏根。 ^3-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-464]]

如是諸道隨其次第， ^3-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-465]]

_此中一切菩提分法，皆由五門而得建立， ^3-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-467]]

謂所緣故、自體故、助伴故、修習故、修果故。 ^3-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-469]]

_四念住所緣者， ^3-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-470]]

謂身、受、心、法。 ^3-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-471]]

復有四事，謂我所依事、 ^3-471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-472]]

我受用事、 ^3-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-473]]

我自體事、 ^3-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-474]]

我染淨事。 ^3-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-475]]

自體者， ^3-475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-476]]

謂慧及念。 ^3-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-477]]

助伴者， ^3-477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-478]]

謂彼相應心心所等。 ^3-478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-479]]

修習者， ^3-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-480]]

謂於內身等，修循身等觀。 ^3-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-481]]

如於內，於外、於內外亦爾。 ^3-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-482]]

內身者， ^3-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-483]]

謂於此身中，所有內色處。 ^3-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-484]]

外身者， ^3-484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-485]]

謂外所有外色處。 ^3-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-486]]

內外身者， ^3-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-487]]

謂內處相應所有外處根所依止。 ^3-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-488]]

又他身中，所有內色處。 ^3-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-489]]

云何於身修循身觀？ ^3-489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-490]]

謂以分別影像身與本質身平等。 ^3-490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-491]]

隨觀內受者， ^3-491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-492]]

謂因內身所生受。 ^3-492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-493]]

外受者， ^3-493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-494]]

謂因外身所生受。 ^3-494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-495]]

內外受者， ^3-495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-496]]

謂因內外身所生受。 ^3-496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-497]]

如受，心、法亦爾。 ^3-497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-498]]

如於身修循身觀，如是於受等修循受等觀，如其所應。 ^3-498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-499]]

又修習者，謂欲、 ^3-499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-500]]

勤、 ^3-500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-501]]

策、 ^3-501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-502]]

勵、 ^3-502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-503]]

勇猛、 ^3-503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-504]]

不息、 ^3-504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-505]]

正念、 ^3-505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-506]]

正知 ^3-506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-507]]

及不放逸， ^3-507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-508]]

修習差別故。 ^3-508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-509]]

欲修習者，謂為對治不作意隨煩惱。 ^3-509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-510]]

勤修習者，謂為對治懈怠隨煩惱。 ^3-510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-511]]

策修習者，謂為對治惛沈、掉舉隨煩惱。 ^3-511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-512]]

勵修習者，謂為對治心下劣性隨煩惱。 ^3-512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-513]]

勇猛修習者，謂為對治踈漏疲倦隨煩惱。 ^3-513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-514]]

不息修習者，謂為對治得少善法生知足喜隨煩惱。 ^3-514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-515]]

正念修習者，謂為對治忘失尊教隨煩惱。 ^3-515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-516]]

正知修習者，謂為對治毀犯追悔隨煩惱。 ^3-516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-517]]

不放逸修習者，謂為對治捨諸善軛隨煩惱。 ^3-517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-518]]

修果者， ^3-518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-519]]

謂斷四顛倒、 ^3-519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-520]]

趣入四諦、 ^3-520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-521]]

身等離繫。 ^3-521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-522]]

_四正斷所緣者， ^3-522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-523]]

謂已生、未生、所治、能治法。 ^3-523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-524]]

自體者， ^3-524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-525]]

謂精進。 ^3-525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-526]]

助伴者， ^3-526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-527]]

謂彼相應心心所等。 ^3-527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-528]]

修習者， ^3-528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-529]]

如契經說： ^3-529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-530]]

生欲、 ^3-530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-531]]

策勵、 ^3-531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-532]]

發起正勤、 ^3-532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-533]]

策心、 ^3-533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-534]]

持心。 ^3-534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-535]]

此中諸句，顯修正勤及所依止。 ^3-535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-536]]

所依止者，謂欲。 ^3-536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-537]]

正勤者，謂策勵等。 ^3-537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-538]]

於止、舉、捨相作意中， ^3-538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-539]]

為欲損減惛沈、掉舉，發起正勤， ^3-539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-540]]

故次說言策心、持心。 ^3-540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-541]]

修果者， ^3-541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-542]]

謂盡棄捨一切所治。 ^3-542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-543]]

於能對治，若得若增，是名修果。 ^3-543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-544]]

_四神足所緣者， ^3-544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-545]]

謂已成滿定所作事。 ^3-545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-546]]

自體者， ^3-546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-547]]

謂三摩地。 ^3-547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-548]]

助伴者， ^3-548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-549]]

謂欲、勤、心、觀及彼相應心心所等。 ^3-549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-550]]

云何欲三摩地？ ^3-550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-551]]

謂由殷重方便，觸心一境性。 ^3-551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-552]]

云何勤三摩地？ ^3-552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-553]]

謂由無間方便，觸心一境性。 ^3-553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-554]]

云何心三摩地？ ^3-554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-555]]

謂由先修三摩地力，觸心一境性。 ^3-555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-556]]

云何觀三摩地？ ^3-556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-557]]

謂由聞他教法內自簡擇，觸心一境性。 ^3-557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-558]]

又欲三摩地者，謂由生欲，觸心一境性。 ^3-558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-559]]

勤三摩地者，謂由策勵發起正勤，觸心一境性。 ^3-559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-560]]

心三摩地者，謂由持心，觸心一境性。 ^3-560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-561]]

觀三摩地者，謂由策心，觸心一境性。 ^3-561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-562]]

修習者，謂數修習八種斷行。 ^3-562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-563]]

何等為八？ ^3-563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-564]]

謂欲、 ^3-564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-565]]

精進、 ^3-565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-566]]

信、 ^3-566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-567]]

安、 ^3-567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-568]]

正念、 ^3-568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-569]]

正知、 ^3-569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-570]]

思、 ^3-570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-571]]

捨。 ^3-571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-572]]

如是八種略攝為四， ^3-572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-573]]

謂加行、 ^3-573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-574]]

攝受、 ^3-574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-575]]

繼屬、 ^3-575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-576]]

對治。 ^3-576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-577]]

又欲、 ^3-577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-578]]

勤、 ^3-578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-579]]

心、 ^3-579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-580]]

觀， ^3-580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-581]]

修有二種， ^3-581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-582]]

謂并因緣聚散遠離修、 ^3-582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-583]]

不劣、 ^3-583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-584]]

不散、 ^3-584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-585]]

彼二所依隨順修。 ^3-585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-586]]

修果者， ^3-586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-587]]

謂已善修治三摩地故， ^3-587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-588]]

隨所欲證所通達法，即能隨心通達變現。又於別別處所法中，證得堪能自在作用， ^3-588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-589]]

如所願樂能辦種種神通等事，又能引發勝品功德。 ^3-589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-590]]

五根所緣者， ^3-590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-591]]

謂四聖諦。 ^3-591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-592]]

自體者， ^3-592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-593]]

謂信、 ^3-593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-594]]

精進、 ^3-594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-595]]

念、 ^3-595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-596]]

定、 ^3-596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-597]]

慧。 ^3-597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-598]]

助伴者， ^3-598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-599]]

謂彼相應心心所等。 ^3-599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-600]]

修習者， ^3-600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-601]]

謂信根於諸諦，起忍可行修習。 ^3-601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-602]]

精進根於諸諦生忍可已，為覺悟故，起精進行修習。 ^3-602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-603]]

念根於諸諦發精進已，繫念起不忘失行修習。 ^3-603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-604]]

定根於諸諦既繫念已，起心一境性行修習。 ^3-604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-605]]

慧根於諸諦心既得定，起簡擇行修習。 ^3-605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-606]]

修果者， ^3-606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-607]]

謂能速發諦現觀， ^3-607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-608]]

及能修治煖、頂，引發忍、世第一法。 ^3-608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-609]]

_如五根，五力亦爾。 ^3-609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-610]]

差別者， ^3-610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-611]]

由此能損減所對治障，不可屈伏，故名為力。 ^3-611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-612]]

_七覺支所緣者， ^3-612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-613]]

謂四聖諦如實性。 ^3-613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-614]]

自體者， ^3-614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-615]]

謂念、 ^3-615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-616]]

擇法、 ^3-616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-617]]

精進、 ^3-617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-618]]

喜、 ^3-618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-619]]

安、 ^3-619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-620]]

定、 ^3-620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-621]]

捨。 ^3-621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-622]]

念是所依支。 ^3-622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-623]]

擇法是自體支。 ^3-623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-624]]

精進是出離支。 ^3-624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-625]]

喜是利益支。 ^3-625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-626]]

安、定、捨是不染污支， ^3-626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-627]]

由此不染污故、 ^3-627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-628]]

依止不染污故、 ^3-628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-629]]

體是不染污故。 ^3-629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-630]]

助伴者， ^3-630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-631]]

謂彼相應心心所等。 ^3-631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-632]]

修習者， ^3-632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-633]]

修念覺支。 ^3-633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-634]]

謂依止遠離、 ^3-634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-635]]

依止無欲、 ^3-635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-636]]

依止寂滅， ^3-636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-637]]

迴向棄捨。 ^3-637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-638]]

如念覺支，乃至捨覺支亦爾。 ^3-638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-640]]

如是四句，次第顯示緣四諦境，修習覺支。 ^3-640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-641]]

修果者， ^3-641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-642]]

謂見所斷煩惱永斷。 ^3-642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-643]]

_八聖道支所緣者， ^3-643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-644]]

謂即此後時，四聖諦如實性。 ^3-644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-645]]

自體者， ^3-645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-647]]

謂正見、 ^3-647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-648]]

正思惟、 ^3-648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-649]]

正語、 ^3-649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-650]]

正業、 ^3-650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-651]]

正命、 ^3-651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-652]]

正精進、 ^3-652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-653]]

正念、 ^3-653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-654]]

正定。 ^3-654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-655]]

正見是分別支。 ^3-655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-656]]

正思惟是誨示他支。 ^3-656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-657]]

正語、正業、正命是令他信支， ^3-657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-658]]

見、戒、命清淨性故。 ^3-658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-659]]

正精進是淨煩惱障支。 ^3-659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-660]]

正念是淨隨煩惱障支。 ^3-660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-661]]

正定是能淨最勝功德障支。 ^3-661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-662]]

助伴者， ^3-662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-663]]

謂彼相應心心所等。 ^3-663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-664]]

修習者，如覺支說。 ^3-664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-665]]

修果者， ^3-665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-667]]

謂分別誨示他 ^3-667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-668]]

令他信， ^3-668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-669]]

煩惱障淨、 ^3-669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-670]]

隨煩惱障淨、 ^3-670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-671]]

最勝功德障淨故。 ^3-671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-672]]

_四種正行者， ^3-672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-673]]

謂苦遲通行、 ^3-673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-674]]

苦速通行、 ^3-674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-675]]

樂遲通行、 ^3-675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-676]]

樂速通行。 ^3-676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-677]]

初謂鈍根未得根本靜慮， ^3-677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-678]]

第二謂利根未得根本靜慮， ^3-678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-679]]

第三謂鈍根已得根本靜慮， ^3-679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-680]]

第四謂利根已得根本靜慮。 ^3-680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-681]]

四種法迹者， ^3-681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-682]]

謂無貪、 ^3-682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-683]]

無瞋、 ^3-683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-684]]

正念、 ^3-684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-685]]

正定。 ^3-685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-686]]

無貪、無瞋能令增上戒學清淨， ^3-686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-687]]

正念能令增上心學清淨， ^3-687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-688]]

正定能令增上慧學清淨。 ^3-688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-689]]

奢摩他者， ^3-689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-691]]

謂於內攝心令住、 ^3-691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-692]]

等住、 ^3-692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-693]]

安住、 ^3-693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-694]]

近住、 ^3-694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-695]]

調順、 ^3-695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-696]]

寂靜、 ^3-696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-697]]

最極寂靜、 ^3-697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-698]]

專注一趣、 ^3-698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-699]]

平等攝持。 ^3-699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-700]]

毘鉢舍那者， ^3-700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-702]]

謂簡擇諸法、 ^3-702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-703]]

最極簡擇、 ^3-703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-704]]

普遍尋思、 ^3-704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-705]]

周審觀察， ^3-705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-706]]

為欲對治麁重、相結故、 ^3-706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-707]]

為欲制伏諸顛倒故、 ^3-707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-708]]

令無倒心善安住故。 ^3-708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-709]]

_又依奢摩他、毘鉢舍那立四種道。 ^3-709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-710]]

或有一類，已得奢摩他，非毘鉢舍那， ^3-710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-711]]

此類依奢摩他進修毘鉢舍那。 ^3-711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-712]]

或有一類，已得毘鉢舍那，非奢摩他， ^3-712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-713]]

此類依毘鉢舍那，進修奢摩他。 ^3-713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-714]]

或有一類，不得奢摩他，亦非毘鉢舍那， ^3-714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-715]]

此類專心制伏惛沈、掉舉，雙修二道。 ^3-715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-716]]

或有一類，已得奢摩他及毘鉢舍那， ^3-716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-717]]

此類奢摩他、毘鉢舍那，二道和合平等雙轉。 ^3-717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-718]]

_三根者， ^3-718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-719]]

謂未知當知根、 ^3-719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-720]]

已知根、 ^3-720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-721]]

具知根。 ^3-721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-722]]

云何未知當知根？ ^3-722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-723]]

謂於加行道及於見道十五心剎那中，所有諸根。 ^3-723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-724]]

云何已知根？ ^3-724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-725]]

謂從第十六見道心剎那已上，於一切有學道中所有諸根。 ^3-725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-726]]

云何具知根？ ^3-726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-727]]

謂於無學道，所有諸根。 ^3-727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-728]]

_依初靜慮地現修道時，亦修欲界繫所有善根， ^3-728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-729]]

於彼得自在故。 ^3-729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-730]]

如依初靜慮地修欲界善根，如是依一切上地現修道時， ^3-730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-731]]

皆能修習下界下地所有善根， ^3-731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-732]]

於彼得自在故。 ^3-732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-733]]

_何等究竟道？ ^3-733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-734]]

一切麁重永已息故、 ^3-734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-736]]

一切繫得永已斷故、 ^3-736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-737]]

永證一切離繫得故， ^3-737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-738]]

謂依金剛喻定， ^3-738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-739]]

從此次第無間轉依， ^3-739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-740]]

證得盡智及無生智 ^3-740

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-741]]

十無學法等。 何等為十？ ^3-741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-742]]

謂無學正見， ^3-742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-743]]

乃至無學正定、 ^3-743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-744]]

無學正解脫、 ^3-744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-745]]

無學正智。 ^3-745

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-746]]

如是等法名究竟道。 ^3-746

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-747]]

_云何名為一切麁重？ ^3-747

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-748]]

略說有二十四種， ^3-748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-749]]

謂一切遍行戲論麁重、 ^3-749

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-750]]

領受麁重、 ^3-750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-751]]

煩惱麁重、 ^3-751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-752]]

業麁重、 ^3-752

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-753]]

異熟麁重、 ^3-753

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-754]]

煩惱障麁重、 ^3-754

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-755]]

業障麁重、 ^3-755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-756]]

異熟障麁重、 ^3-756

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-757]]

蓋麁重、 ^3-757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-758]]

尋思麁重、 ^3-758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-759]]

飲食麁重、 ^3-759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-760]]

交會麁重、 ^3-760

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-761]]

夢麁重、 ^3-761

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-762]]

病麁重、 ^3-762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-763]]

老麁重、 ^3-763

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-764]]

死麁重、 ^3-764

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-765]]

勞倦麁重、 ^3-765

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-766]]

堅固麁重、 ^3-766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-767]]

麁麁重、 ^3-767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-768]]

中麁重、 ^3-768

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-769]]

細麁重、 ^3-769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-770]]

煩惱障麁重、 ^3-770

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-771]]

定障麁重、 ^3-771

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-772]]

所知障麁重。 ^3-772

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-773]]

_云何繫得？ ^3-773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-774]]

謂於麁重積集假立繫得性。 ^3-774

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-775]]

云何離繫得？ ^3-775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-776]]

謂於麁重離散假立離繫得性。 ^3-776

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-777]]

_云何金剛喻定？ ^3-777

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-778]]

謂居修道最後斷結道位，所有三摩地， ^3-778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-779]]

或加行道攝、 ^3-779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-780]]

或無間道攝。 ^3-780

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-781]]

加行道攝者，謂從此已去，非一切障所礙，能破一切障。 ^3-781
