何等貪行補特伽羅？ ^4-52

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-53]]

謂有猛利長時貪欲。 ^4-53

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-54]]

如是瞋行、癡行、慢行， ^4-54

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-55]]

及尋思行補特伽羅，皆有猛利長時差別。 ^4-55

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-56]]

何等等分行補特伽羅？ ^4-56

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-57]]

謂住自性位煩惱。 ^4-57

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-58]]

何等薄塵行補特伽羅？ ^4-58

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-59]]

謂住自性位微薄煩惱。 ^4-59

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-60]]

何等聲聞乘補特伽羅？ ^4-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-61]]

謂住聲聞法性，若定、若不定性，是鈍根。 ^4-61

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-62]]

自求解脫發弘正願， ^4-62

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-63]]

修厭離貪，解脫意樂， ^4-63

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-64]]

以聲聞藏為所緣境，精進修行法隨法行，得盡苦際。 ^4-64

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-65]]

何等獨覺乘補特伽羅？ ^4-65

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-66]]

謂住獨覺法性，若定、若不定性，是中根。 ^4-66

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-67]]

自求解脫發弘正願， ^4-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-68]]

修厭離貪，解脫意樂， ^4-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-69]]

及修獨證菩提意樂， ^4-69

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-70]]

即聲聞藏為所緣境，精進修行法隨法行， ^4-70

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-71]]

或先未起順決擇分、 ^4-71

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-72]]

或先已起順決擇分、 ^4-72

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-73]]

或先未得果、或先已得果， ^4-73

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-74]]

出無佛世，唯內思惟聖道現前，或如麟角獨住， ^4-74

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-75]]

或復獨勝部行得盡苦際。 ^4-75

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-76]]

何等大乘補特伽羅？ ^4-76

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-77]]

謂住菩薩法性，若定、若不定性、是利根。 ^4-77

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-78]]

為求解脫一切有情，發弘正願， ^4-78

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-79]]

修無住處涅槃意樂， ^4-79

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-80]]

以菩薩藏為所緣境，精進修行法隨法行， ^4-80

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-81]]

成熟眾生修淨佛土，得受大記， ^4-81

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-82]]

證成無上正等菩提。 ^4-82

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-83]]

何等未具資糧補特伽羅？ ^4-83

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-84]]

謂緣諦增上法為境，發起軟品清信勝解， ^4-84

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-85]]

成就軟品順解脫分，未定生時。 ^4-85

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-86]]

何等已具未具資糧補特伽羅？ ^4-86

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-87]]

謂緣諦增上法為境，發起中品清信勝解， ^4-87

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-88]]

成就中品順解脫分，已定生時。 ^4-88

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-89]]

何等已具資糧補特伽羅？ ^4-89

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-90]]

謂緣諦增上法為境，發起上品清信勝解， ^4-90

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-91]]

成就上品順解脫分，即此生時。 ^4-91

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-92]]

又未具資糧者，謂緣諦增上法為境，於諸諦中，成就下品諦察法忍， ^4-92

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-93]]

成就下品順決擇分，未定生時。 ^4-93

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-94]]

已具未具資糧者，謂緣諦增上法為境， ^4-94

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-95]]

於諸諦中，成就中品諦察法忍， ^4-95

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-96]]

成就中品順決擇分，已定生時。 ^4-96

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-97]]

已具資糧者，謂緣諦增上法為境，於諸諦中成就上品諦察法忍， ^4-97

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-98]]

成就上品順決擇分，即此生時。此中三品順決擇分者，謂除世第一法。 ^4-98

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-99]]

由此世第一法性，唯一剎那必不相續，即此生時。 ^4-99

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-100]]

定入現觀非前位故。 ^4-100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-101]]

從下中品順解脫分、順決擇分有可退義，此唯退現行，非退習氣， ^4-101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-102]]

已依涅槃先起善根者不復新發起故。 ^4-102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-103]]

何等隨信行補特伽羅？ ^4-103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-104]]

謂資糧已具、性是鈍根，隨順他教，修諦現觀。 ^4-104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-105]]

何等隨法行補特伽羅？ ^4-105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-106]]

謂資糧已具、性是利根，自然隨順諦增上法，修諦現觀。 ^4-106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-107]]

何等信勝解補特伽羅？ ^4-107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-108]]

謂隨信行已至果位。 ^4-108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-109]]

何等見至補特伽羅？ ^4-109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-110]]

謂隨法行已至果位。 ^4-110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-111]]

何等身證補特伽羅？ ^4-111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-112]]

謂諸有學，已具證得八解脫定。 ^4-112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-113]]

何等慧解脫補特伽羅？ ^4-113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-114]]

謂已盡諸漏，而未具證八解脫定。 ^4-114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-115]]

何等俱分解脫補特伽羅？ ^4-115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-116]]

謂已盡諸漏，及具證得八解脫定。 ^4-116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-117]]

何等預流向補特伽羅？ ^4-117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-118]]

謂住順決擇分位，及住見道十五心剎那位。 ^4-118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-119]]

何等預流果補特伽羅？ ^4-119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-120]]

謂住見道第十六心剎那位。 ^4-120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-121]]

即此見道，亦名趣入正性決定， ^4-121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-122]]

亦名於法現觀。 ^4-122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-123]]

若於欲界未離欲者，後入正性決定位，得預流果。 ^4-123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-124]]

若於欲界倍離欲者，後入正性決定位，得一來果。 ^4-124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-125]]

若已離欲界欲者，後入正性決定位，得不還果。 ^4-125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-126]]

若已永斷見道所斷一切煩惱，得預流果， ^4-126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-127]]

何故但言永斷三結，得預流果？ ^4-127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-128]]

最勝攝故。 ^4-128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-129]]

何故最勝？ ^4-129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-130]]

以於解脫是不發趣因故，雖已發趣，復為邪出離因故，及為不正出離因故。 ^4-130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-131]]

又此三結，是迷所知境因故、迷見因故、迷對治因故。 ^4-131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-132]]

何等一來向補特伽羅？ ^4-132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-133]]

謂於修道中，已斷欲界五品煩惱，安住彼道。 ^4-133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-134]]

何等一來果補特伽羅？ ^4-134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-135]]

謂於修道中，已斷欲界第六品煩惱，安住彼道。 ^4-135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-136]]

何等不還向補特伽羅？ ^4-136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-137]]

謂於修道中，已斷欲界第七第八品煩惱，安住彼道。 ^4-137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-138]]

何等不還果補特伽羅？ ^4-138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-139]]

謂於修道中，已斷欲界第九品煩惱，安住彼道。 ^4-139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-140]]

若已永斷一切見道所斷煩惱，及已永斷欲界修道所斷一切煩惱，得不還果， ^4-140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-141]]

何故但言永斷五順下分結得不還果？ ^4-141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-142]]

最勝攝故。 ^4-142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-143]]

何故最勝？ ^4-143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-144]]

能為下趣、下界勝因故。 ^4-144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-145]]

何等阿羅漢向補特伽羅？ ^4-145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-146]]

謂已永斷有頂八品煩惱，安住彼道。 ^4-146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-147]]

何等阿羅漢果補特伽羅？ ^4-147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-148]]

謂已永斷有頂第九品煩惱，安住彼究竟道。 ^4-148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-149]]

若阿羅漢永斷三界一切煩惱， ^4-149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-150]]

何故但言永斷五順上分結得阿羅漢果？ ^4-150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-151]]

最勝攝故。 ^4-151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-152]]

何故最勝？ ^4-152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-153]]

是取上分因，及不捨上分因故。 ^4-153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-154]]

何等極七返有補特伽羅？ ^4-154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-155]]

謂即預流果，於人天生往來雜受，極至七返，得盡苦際。 ^4-155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-156]]

何等家家補特伽羅？ ^4-156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-157]]

謂即預流果，或於天上或於人中，從家至家，得盡苦際。 ^4-157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-158]]

何等一間補特伽羅？ ^4-158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-159]]

謂即一來果，或於天上唯受一有，得盡苦際。 ^4-159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-160]]

何等中般涅槃補特伽羅？ ^4-160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-161]]

謂生結已斷、起結未斷， ^4-161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-162]]

或中有纔起，即便聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^4-162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-163]]

或中有起已為趣生有，纔起思惟即便聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^4-163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-164]]

或思惟已發趣生有，未到生有即便聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^4-164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-165]]

何等生般涅槃補特伽羅？ ^4-165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-166]]

謂二結俱未斷，纔生色界已即便聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^4-166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-167]]

何等無行般涅槃？ ^4-167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-168]]

謂生彼已，不由加行聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^4-168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-169]]

何等有行般涅槃補特伽羅？ ^4-169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-170]]

謂生彼已，由加行力聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^4-170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-171]]

何等上流補特伽羅？ ^4-171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-172]]

謂於色界地，地中皆受生已，乃至最後入色究竟，於彼無漏聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^4-172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-173]]

復有乃至往到有頂，聖道現前得盡苦際。 ^4-173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-174]]

又雜修第四靜慮有五品差別： ^4-174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-175]]

一下品修、二中品修、三上品修、 ^4-175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-176]]

四上勝品修、五上極品修。 由此五品雜修第四靜慮故，如其次第生五淨居。 ^4-176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-177]]

何等退法阿羅漢？ ^4-177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-178]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散若不遊散， ^4-178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-179]]

若思惟若不思惟，皆可退失現法樂住。 ^4-179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-180]]

何等思法阿羅漢？ ^4-180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-181]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散若不遊散， ^4-181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-182]]

若不思惟，即可退失現法樂住，若思惟已，能不退失。 ^4-182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-183]]

何等護法阿羅漢？ ^4-183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-184]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散便可退失現法樂住， ^4-184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-185]]

若不遊散即能不退。 ^4-185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-186]]

何等住不動阿羅漢？ ^4-186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-187]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散、若不遊散， ^4-187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-188]]

皆能不退現法樂住， ^4-188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-189]]

亦不能練根。 ^4-189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-190]]

何等堪達阿羅漢？ ^4-190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-191]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散若不遊散， ^4-191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-192]]

皆能不退現法樂住， ^4-192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-193]]

然堪能練根。 ^4-193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-194]]

何等不動法阿羅漢？ ^4-194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-195]]

謂利根性，若遊散、若不遊散， ^4-195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-196]]

皆能不退現法樂住。 ^4-196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-197]]

何等欲界異生補特伽羅？ ^4-197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-198]]

謂於欲界，若生若長，不得聖法。 ^4-198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-199]]

何等欲界有學補特伽羅？ ^4-199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-200]]

謂於欲界若生若長，已得聖法，猶有餘結。 ^4-200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-201]]

何等欲界無學補特伽羅？ ^4-201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-202]]

謂於欲界若生若長，已得聖法，無有餘結。 ^4-202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-203]]

如欲界有三，色、無色亦爾。 ^4-203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-204]]

何等欲色界菩薩補特伽羅？ ^4-204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-205]]

謂與滅離無色界生靜慮相， ^4-205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-206]]

應住靜慮樂而生欲界，或生色界。 ^4-206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-207]]

何等欲界獨覺補特伽羅？ ^4-207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-208]]

謂無佛出世時生於欲界，自然證得獨覺菩提。 ^4-208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-209]]

何等不可思議如來補特伽羅？ ^4-209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-210]]

謂且於欲界，始從示現安住覩史多天妙寶宮殿，乃至示現大般涅槃，示現一切諸佛菩薩所行大行。 ^4-210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-211]]

何等勝解行菩薩補特伽羅？ ^4-211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-212]]

謂住勝解行地中，成就菩薩下、中、上忍。 ^4-212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-213]]

何等增上意樂行菩薩補特伽羅？ ^4-213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-214]]

謂十地中所有菩薩。 ^4-214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-215]]

何等有相行菩薩補特伽羅？ ^4-215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-216]]

謂住極喜、離垢、發光、焰慧、極難勝、現前地中所有菩薩。 ^4-216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-217]]

何等無相行菩薩補特伽羅？ ^4-217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-218]]

謂住遠行地中所有菩薩。 ^4-218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-219]]

何等無功用行菩薩補特伽羅？ ^4-219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-220]]

謂住不動、善慧、法雲地中所有菩薩。 ^4-220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-221]]

復次，如說預流果補特伽羅，此有二種：一漸出離、二頓出離。 ^4-221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-222]]

漸出離者，如前廣說。 ^4-222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-223]]

頓出離者，謂入諦現觀已， ^4-223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-224]]

依止未至定發出世間道，頓斷三界一切煩惱， ^4-224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-225]]

品品別斷唯立二果，謂預流果、阿羅漢果。 ^4-225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-226]]

如是補特伽羅，多於現法或臨終時，善辨聖旨； ^4-226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-227]]

設不能辨，由願力故，即以願力還生欲界，出無佛世成獨勝果。 ^4-227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-228]]

大乘阿毘達磨集論卷第六 大乘阿毘達磨集論卷第七 無著菩薩造 三藏法師玄奘奉 詔譯 決擇分中得品第三之二 ^4-228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-229]]

云何建立現觀？ ^4-229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-230]]

略有十種， ^4-230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-231]]

謂法現觀、義現觀、 ^4-231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-232]]

真現觀、後現觀、 ^4-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-233]]

寶現觀、不行現觀、 ^4-233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-234]]

究竟現觀、聲聞現觀、 ^4-234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-235]]

獨覺現觀、菩薩現觀。 ^4-235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-236]]

何等法現觀？ ^4-236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-237]]

謂於諸諦增上法中，已得上品淨信勝解，隨信而行。 ^4-237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-238]]

何等義現觀？ ^4-238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-239]]

謂於諸諦增上法中，已得上品諦察法忍，此忍居順決擇分位， ^4-239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-240]]

此由三種如理作意所顯發故，復成三品，謂上軟、上中、上上。 ^4-240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-241]]

何等真現觀？ ^4-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-242]]

謂已得見道十六心剎那位所有聖道。 ^4-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-243]]

又見道中，得現觀邊安立諦，世俗智不現在前， ^4-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-244]]

於修道位，此世俗智方可現前。 ^4-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-245]]

何等後現觀？ 謂一切修道。 ^4-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-246]]

何等寶現觀？ ^4-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-247]]

謂於佛證淨、於法證淨、於僧證淨。 ^4-247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-248]]

何等不行現觀？ ^4-248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-249]]

謂已證得無作律儀，雖居學位， ^4-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-250]]

而謂我今已盡那落迦、已盡傍生、 ^4-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-251]]

已盡餓鬼、已盡顛墜惡趣。 ^4-251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-252]]

我不復造惡趣業，感惡趣異熟。 ^4-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-253]]

何等究竟現觀？ ^4-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-254]]

如道諦中，究竟道說。 ^4-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-255]]

何等聲聞現觀？ ^4-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-256]]

謂前所說七種現觀，從聞他音而證得故，名聲聞現觀。 ^4-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-257]]

何等獨覺現觀？ ^4-257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-258]]

謂前所說七種現觀，不由他音而證得故，名獨覺現觀。 ^4-258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-259]]

何等菩薩現觀？ ^4-259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-260]]

謂諸菩薩於前所說七現觀中，起修習忍而不作證， ^4-260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-261]]

然於菩薩極喜地中，入諸菩薩正性決定，是名菩薩現觀。 ^4-261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-262]]

聲聞現觀、菩薩現觀有何差別？ ^4-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-263]]

略說有十一種， ^4-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-264]]

謂境界差別、任持差別、 ^4-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-265]]

通達差別、誓願差別、 ^4-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-266]]

出離差別、攝受差別、 ^4-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-267]]

建立差別、眷屬差別、勝生差別、 ^4-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-268]]

生差別、果差別。 ^4-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-269]]

其果差別復有十種， ^4-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-270]]

謂轉依差別、功德圓滿差別、五相差別、 ^4-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-271]]

三身差別、涅槃差別、 ^4-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-272]]

證得和合智用差別、障清淨差別、 ^4-272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-273]]

和合作業差別、方便示現成等正覺入般涅槃差別、 ^4-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-274]]

五種拔濟差別。 ^4-274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-275]]

諸無量等最勝功德何現觀攝？ ^4-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-276]]

後現觀、究竟現觀攝。 ^4-276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-277]]

彼復云何？ 謂無量、解脫、 ^4-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-278]]

勝處、遍處、 ^4-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-279]]

無諍、願智、 ^4-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-280]]

無礙解、神通、 ^4-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-281]]

相隨好、清淨、 ^4-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-282]]

力、無畏、念住、 ^4-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-283]]

不護、無忘失法、永斷習氣、 ^4-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-284]]

大悲、不共佛法、 ^4-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-285]]

一切種妙智，如是等功德，諸契經中處處宣說。 ^4-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-286]]

無量者，謂四無量。 ^4-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-287]]

云何慈？ ^4-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-288]]

謂依止靜慮，於諸有情與樂相應意樂住具足中，若定若慧， ^4-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-289]]

及彼相應諸心心所。 ^4-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-290]]

云何悲？ ^4-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-291]]

謂依止靜慮，於諸有情離苦意樂住具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-292]]

云何喜？ ^4-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-293]]

謂依止靜慮，於諸有情不離樂意樂住具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-294]]

云何捨？ ^4-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-295]]

謂依止靜慮，於諸有情利益意樂住具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-296]]

解脫者，謂八解脫。 ^4-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-297]]

云何有色觀諸色？ ^4-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-298]]

{乃至為解脫變化障。} ^4-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-299]]

謂依止靜慮，於內未伏見者色想，或現安立見者色想， ^4-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-300]]

觀所見色住具足中若定若慧， ^4-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-301]]

及彼相應諸心心所， ^4-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-302]]

乃至為解脫變化障。 ^4-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-303]]

云何內無色想觀外諸色？ ^4-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-304]]

謂依止靜慮，於已伏見者色想，或現安立見者無色想， ^4-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-305]]

觀所見色住具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-306]]

云何淨解脫身作證具足住？ ^4-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-307]]

謂依止靜慮，於內淨不淨諸色，已得展轉相待想、 ^4-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-308]]

展轉相入想、展轉一味想故， ^4-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-309]]

於彼已得住具足中若定若慧，餘如前說， ^4-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-310]]

乃至為解脫淨不淨變化煩惱生起障。 ^4-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-311]]

云何無邊空處解脫？ ^4-311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-312]]

謂於隨順解脫無邊空處住具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-313]]

如無邊空處解脫， ^4-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-314]]

無邊識處、無所有處、非想非非想處解脫亦爾。 ^4-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-315]]

乃至為解脫寂靜解脫無滯礙障。 ^4-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-316]]

云何想受滅解脫？ ^4-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-317]]

{為解脫想受滅障。} ^4-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-318]]

謂依止非想非非想處解脫， ^4-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-319]]

超過諸餘寂靜解脫，住於似真解脫，住具足中心心所滅， ^4-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-320]]

為解脫想受滅障。 ^4-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-321]]

勝處者，謂八勝處。 ^4-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-322]]

前四勝處由二解脫所建立， ^4-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-323]]

後四勝處由一解脫所建立。 ^4-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-324]]

此中解脫是意解所緣， ^4-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-325]]

勝處是勝伏所緣，自在轉故。 ^4-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-326]]

依有情數非有情數說色少多， ^4-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-327]]

依淨不淨說色好惡， ^4-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-328]]

依人與天說色劣勝， ^4-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-329]]

餘如解脫中說。 ^4-329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-330]]

勝伏所緣，故名勝處。 遍處者，謂十遍處，所緣遍滿，故名遍處。 於其遍滿住具足中若定若慧， ^4-330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-331]]

及彼相應心心所法，是名遍處。 ^4-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-332]]

何故於遍處建立地等？ ^4-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-333]]

由此遍處，觀所依能依色皆遍滿故， ^4-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-334]]

餘隨所應如解脫說。 ^4-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-335]]

如是遍處能成滿解脫。 ^4-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-337]]

無諍者，謂依止靜慮，於防護他所應起煩惱住具足中若定若慧， ^4-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-338]]

及彼相應諸心心所。 ^4-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-340]]

願智者，謂依止靜慮，於為了所知願具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-341]]

無礙解者，謂四無礙解。 ^4-341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-342]]

云何法無礙解？ ^4-342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-343]]

謂依止靜慮，於一切法名差別，無礙具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-344]]

云何義無礙解？ ^4-344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-345]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-346]]

於諸相及意趣，無礙具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-347]]

云何訓詞無礙解？ ^4-347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-348]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-349]]

於諸方言音及訓釋諸法言詞，無礙具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-350]]

云何辯才無礙解？ ^4-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-351]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-352]]

於諸法差別，無礙具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-353]]

神通者，謂六神通。 ^4-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-354]]

云何神境通？ ^4-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-355]]

謂依止靜慮，於種種神變威德具足中若定若慧， ^4-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-356]]

及彼相應諸心心所。 ^4-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-357]]

云何天耳通？ ^4-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-358]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-359]]

於隨聞種種音聲威德具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-360]]

云何心差別通？ ^4-360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-361]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-362]]

於入他有情心行差別威德具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-363]]

云何宿住隨念通？ ^4-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-364]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-365]]

於隨念前際所行威德具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-366]]

云何死生通？ ^4-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-367]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-368]]

於觀有情死生差別威德具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-369]]

云何漏盡通？ ^4-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-370]]

謂依止靜慮，於漏盡智威德具足中若定若慧， ^4-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-371]]

及彼相應諸心心所。 ^4-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-373]]

相隨好者，謂依止靜慮，於相隨好莊嚴所依，示現具足中若定若慧， ^4-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-374]]

及彼相應諸心心所，并彼所起異熟。 ^4-374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-375]]

清淨者，謂四清淨。 ^4-375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-376]]

云何依止清淨？ ^4-376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-377]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-378]]

於隨所欲依止取住，捨具足中若定若慧， ^4-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-379]]

及彼相應諸心心所。 ^4-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-380]]

云何境界清淨？ ^4-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-381]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-382]]

於隨所欲境界變化，智具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-383]]

云何心清淨？ ^4-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-384]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-385]]

於如所欲三摩地門，自在具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-386]]

云何智清淨？ ^4-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-387]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-388]]

於隨所欲陀羅尼門，任持具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-389]]

力者，謂如來十力。 ^4-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-390]]

云何處非處智力？ ^4-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-391]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-392]]

於一切種處非處智具足中若定若慧， ^4-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-393]]

及彼相應諸心心所。 ^4-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-394]]

云何自業智力？ ^4-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-395]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-396]]

於一切種，自業智具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-397]]

餘力隨應當知亦爾。 ^4-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-398]]

無畏者，謂四無畏。 ^4-398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-399]]

云何正等覺無畏？ ^4-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-400]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-401]]

由自利門，於一切種所知境界正等覺自稱德號建立具足中若定若慧， ^4-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-402]]

及彼相應諸心心所。 ^4-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-403]]

云何漏盡無畏？ ^4-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-404]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-405]]

由自利門， ^4-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-406]]

於一切種漏盡自稱德號建立具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-407]]

云何障法無畏？ ^4-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-408]]

謂依止靜慮， ^4-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-409]]

由利他門， ^4-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-410]]

於一切種說障礙法自稱德號建立具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-411]]

云何出苦道無畏？ ^4-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-412]]

謂依止靜慮，由利他門， ^4-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-413]]

於一切種說出苦道法自稱德號建立具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-414]]

念住者，即三念住， ^4-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-415]]

謂御大眾時， ^4-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-416]]

於一切種雜染不現行具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-417]]

不護者，即三不護， ^4-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-418]]

謂御大眾時， ^4-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-419]]

於隨所欲，教授教誡方便具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-420]]

無忘失法者， ^4-420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-421]]

謂於一切種，隨其所作所說明記具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-422]]

永斷習氣者， ^4-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-423]]

謂一切智者，於非一切智所作不現行具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-424]]

大悲者， ^4-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-425]]

謂於緣無間苦境大悲住具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-426]]

不共佛法者， ^4-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-427]]

即十八種不共佛法。 ^4-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-428]]

謂於不共身語意業清淨具足中、 ^4-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-429]]

於所依及果根未得不退具足中、 ^4-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-430]]

於不共業現行具足中、 ^4-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-431]]

於不共智住具足中若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^4-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-432]]

一切種妙智者， ^4-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-433]]

謂於蘊界處一切種妙智性具足中若定若慧， ^4-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-434]]

及彼相應諸心心所。 ^4-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-435]]

云何引發如是等功德？ ^4-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-436]]

謂依止清淨四靜慮，若外道、若聲聞、若菩薩等， ^4-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-437]]

引發四無量五神通。 ^4-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-438]]

多分依止邊際第四靜慮，若聲聞、若菩薩、若如來等，引發所餘功德。 ^4-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-439]]

何因引發如是功德？ ^4-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-440]]

謂依止靜慮數數思惟，隨所建立法故。 ^4-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-441]]

如是等功德略有二種： ^4-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-442]]

一現前發起自所作用、二安住自性。 ^4-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-443]]

若現發起自所作用，以出世後所得世俗智為體。 ^4-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-444]]

若安住自性，用出世智為體。 ^4-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-445]]

無量作何業？ 謂捨所治障， ^4-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-446]]

哀愍住故，能速圓滿福德資糧。 ^4-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-447]]

成熟有情心無厭倦。 ^4-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-448]]

解脫作何業？ ^4-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-449]]

謂引發變化事。 ^4-449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-450]]

於淨不淨變化無有艱難。 ^4-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-451]]

於寂靜解脫無有滯礙。 ^4-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-452]]

能住第一寂靜聖住，由勝解思惟故。 ^4-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-453]]

勝處作何業？ ^4-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-454]]

謂能令前三解脫所緣境界自在而轉，由勝伏所緣故。 ^4-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-455]]

遍處作何業？ ^4-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-456]]

謂善能成辦解脫所緣，遍滿流布故。 ^4-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-457]]

無諍作何業？ ^4-457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-458]]

謂所發語言聞皆信伏， ^4-458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-459]]

愛護他心最為勝故，如其所應發語言故。 ^4-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-460]]

願智作何業？ ^4-460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-461]]

謂能善記別三世等事，一切世間咸所恭敬。 ^4-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-462]]

由遠一切眾眾所歸仰故。 ^4-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-463]]

無礙解作何業？ ^4-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-464]]

謂善說法要悅眾生心。 ^4-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-465]]

能絕一切所疑網故。 ^4-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-466]]

神通作何業？ ^4-466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-467]]

謂以身業語業記心，化導有情令入聖教。 ^4-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-468]]

善知有情一切心行及過未已，如應教授令永出離。 ^4-468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-469]]

相及隨好作何業？ ^4-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-470]]

謂能令暫見，謂大丈夫心生淨信。 ^4-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-471]]

清淨作何業？ ^4-471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-472]]

謂由此勢力故取生有。隨其樂欲，或住一劫、或復劫餘。 ^4-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-473]]

或捨壽行。 ^4-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-474]]

或於諸法自在而轉。 ^4-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-475]]

或於諸定自在而轉。 ^4-475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-476]]

或復任持諸佛正法。 ^4-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-477]]

力作何業？ ^4-477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-478]]

謂為除捨無因惡因論、不作而得論，無倒宣說增上生道。 ^4-478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-479]]

悟入一切有情心行、 ^4-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-480]]

正說法品、 ^4-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-481]]

意樂、隨眠、境界、資糧、 ^4-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-482]]

當能、出離，隨其所應宣說決定勝道。 ^4-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-483]]

降伏諸魔。 ^4-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-484]]

善能記別一切問論。 ^4-484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-485]]

無畏作何業？ ^4-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-486]]

謂處大眾中，自正建立我為大師。 ^4-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-487]]

摧伏一切邪難外道。 ^4-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-488]]

念住作何業？ ^4-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-489]]

謂能不染污攝御大眾。 ^4-489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-490]]

不護作何業？ ^4-490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-491]]

謂能無間斷教授教誡所化徒眾。 ^4-491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-492]]

無忘失法作何業？ ^4-492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-493]]

謂能不捨離一切佛事。 ^4-493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-494]]

永斷習氣作何業？ ^4-494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-495]]

謂離諸煩惱，亦不顯現似諸煩惱所作事業。 ^4-495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-496]]

大悲作何業？ ^4-496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-497]]

謂日夜六時遍觀世間。 ^4-497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-498]]

不共佛法作何業？ ^4-498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-499]]

謂由身語意業清淨已得不退， ^4-499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-500]]

若行若住，映蔽一切聽聞、獨覺。 ^4-500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-501]]

一切種妙智作何業？ ^4-501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-502]]

謂能絕一切有情一切疑網， ^4-502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-503]]

令正法眼長時得住， ^4-503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-504]]

由此有情未成熟者，令其成熟；已成熟者令得解脫。 ^4-504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-505]]

於上所說現觀位中，證得後後勝品道時，捨前所得下劣品道。 ^4-505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-506]]

又即此時集斷。 ^4-506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-507]]

作證於無餘依涅槃界位，聲聞獨覺一切聖道，無不皆由頓捨所捨，非諸菩薩， ^4-507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-508]]

是故唯說諸菩薩等為無盡善根者、無盡功德者。 ^4-508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-509]]

何故建立諸無記事？ ^4-509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-510]]

由彼所問不如理故。 ^4-510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-511]]

何故所問不如理耶？ ^4-511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-512]]

遠離因、果、染、淨所應思處故。 ^4-512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-513]]

何緣菩薩已入菩薩超昇離生位，而非預流耶？ ^4-513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-514]]

由得不住道，一向預流行不成就故。 ^4-514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-515]]

何緣亦非一來耶？ ^4-515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-516]]

故受諸有無量生故。 ^4-516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-517]]

何緣亦非不還耶？ ^4-517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-518]]

安住靜慮還生欲界故。 ^4-518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-519]]

又諸菩薩已得諦現觀， ^4-519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-520]]

於十地修道位，唯修所知障對治道，非煩惱障對治道。 ^4-520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-521]]

若得菩提時，頓斷煩惱障及所知障。 ^4-521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-522]]

頓成阿羅漢及如來。 ^4-522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-523]]

此諸菩薩雖未永斷一切煩惱， ^4-523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-524]]

然此煩惱猶如呪藥所伏諸毒，不起一切煩惱過失，一切地中如阿羅漢已斷煩惱。 ^4-524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-525]]

又諸菩薩於所知境應修善巧，於諸方便應修善巧， ^4-525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-526]]

於虛妄分別應修善巧，於無分別應修善巧。 ^4-526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-527]]

於時時中應修練根。 ^4-527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-528]]

云何所知境？ ^4-528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-529]]

謂略有六種：一迷亂、二迷亂所依、三不迷亂所依、 ^4-529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-530]]

四迷亂不迷亂、五不迷亂、六不迷亂等流。 ^4-530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-531]]

云何方便善巧？ ^4-531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-532]]

謂略有四種：一成熟有情方便善巧、二圓滿佛法方便善巧、 ^4-532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-533]]

三速證通慧方便善巧、四道無斷絕方便善巧。 ^4-533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-534]]

云何虛妄分別？ ^4-534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-535]]

謂略有十種：一根本分別、二相分別、 ^4-535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-536]]

三相顯現分別、四相變異分別、 ^4-536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-537]]

五相顯現變異分別、六他引分別、 ^4-537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-538]]

七不如理分別、八如理分別、 ^4-538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-539]]

九執著分別、十散亂分別。 ^4-539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-540]]

此復十種，一無性分別、二有性分別、 ^4-540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-541]]

三增益分別、四損減分別、 ^4-541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-542]]

五一性分別、六異性分別、 ^4-542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-543]]

七自性分別、八差別分別、 ^4-543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-544]]

九隨名義分別、十隨義名分別。 ^4-544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-545]]

云何無分別？ ^4-545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-546]]

謂略有三種：一知足無分別、二無顛倒無分別、三無戲論無分別。 ^4-546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-547]]

如此三種，異生、聲聞、菩薩如其次第應知。 ^4-547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-548]]

其相無戲論無分別，復離五相：一非無作意故、 ^4-548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-549]]

二非超過作意故、 ^4-549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-550]]

三非寂靜故、 ^4-550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-551]]

四非自性故、 ^4-551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-552]]

五非於所緣作加行故， ^4-552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-553]]

謂於所緣不起加行。 ^4-553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-554]]

若諸菩薩性是利根，云何復令修練根行？ ^4-554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-555]]

謂令依利軟根，引發利中根， ^4-555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-556]]

復依利中根，引發利利根故。 ^4-556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-557]]

大乘阿毘達磨集論決擇分中論議品第四 ^4-557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-558]]

云何論議決擇？ ^4-558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-559]]

略說有七種： ^4-559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-560]]

謂義決擇、釋決擇、分別顯示決擇、 ^4-560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-561]]

等論決擇、攝決擇、論軌決擇、祕密決擇。 ^4-561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-562]]

何等義決擇？ ^4-562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-563]]

謂依六義而起決擇。 ^4-563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-564]]

何等六義？ ^4-564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-565]]

謂自性義、因義、果義、業義、相應義、轉義。 ^4-565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-566]]

自性義者，謂三自性。 ^4-566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-567]]

因義者，謂三因：一生因、二轉因、三成因。 ^4-567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-568]]

果義者，謂五果：一異熟果、二等流果、三增上果、四事用果、五離繫果。 ^4-568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-569]]

業義者，謂五業：一取受業、 ^4-569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-570]]

二作用業、 ^4-570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-571]]

三加行業、 ^4-571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-572]]

四轉變業、 ^4-572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-573]]

五證得業。 ^4-573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-574]]

相應義者，謂五相應：一聚結相應、 ^4-574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-575]]

二隨逐相應、 ^4-575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-576]]

三連綴相應、 ^4-576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-577]]

四分位相應、 ^4-577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-578]]

五轉變相應。 ^4-578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-579]]

轉義者，謂五轉：一相轉、 ^4-579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-580]]

二安住轉、 ^4-580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-581]]

三顛倒轉、 ^4-581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-582]]

四不顛倒轉、 ^4-582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-583]]

五差別轉。 ^4-583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-584]]

何等釋決擇？ ^4-584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-585]]

謂能解釋諸經宗要。 ^4-585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-586]]

此復云何？ ^4-586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-587]]

略有六種： ^4-587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-588]]

一所遍知事、 ^4-588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-589]]

二所遍知義、 ^4-589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-590]]

三遍知因緣、 ^4-590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-591]]

四遍知自性、 ^4-591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-592]]

五遍知果、 ^4-592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-593]]

六彼證受。 ^4-593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-594]]

又十四門辯釋決擇。 ^4-594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-595]]

何等十四？ ^4-595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-596]]

謂攝釋門、攝事門、 ^4-596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-597]]

總別分門、後後開引門、 ^4-597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-598]]

遮止門、轉變字門、 ^4-598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-599]]

壞不壞門、安立補特伽羅門、 ^4-599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-600]]

安立差別門、理趣門、 ^4-600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-601]]

遍知等門、力無力門、 ^4-601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-602]]

別別引門、引發門。 ^4-602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-603]]

何等分別顯示決擇？ ^4-603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-604]]

謂於如所說蘊等諸法中，隨其所應， ^4-604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-605]]

作一行順前句、順後句、 ^4-605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-606]]

二句、三句、四句、述可句遮止句等。 ^4-606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-607]]

何等等論決擇？ ^4-607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-608]]

謂依八。 ^4-608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-609]]

何八？ ^4-609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-610]]

「若」之詞問答決擇一切真偽。 ^4-610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-611]]

復有四種等論決擇道理： ^4-611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-612]]

一能破、 ^4-612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-613]]

二能立、 ^4-613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-614]]

三能斷、 ^4-614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-615]]

四能覺。 ^4-615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-616]]

何等攝決擇？ ^4-616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-617]]

謂由十處攝諸決擇。 ^4-617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-618]]

何等十處？ ^4-618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-619]]

一成所作決擇處、 ^4-619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-620]]

二趣入決擇處、 ^4-620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-621]]

三勝解決擇處、 ^4-621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-622]]

四道理決擇處、 ^4-622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-623]]

五論決擇處、 ^4-623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-624]]

六通達決擇處、 ^4-624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-625]]

七清淨決擇處、 ^4-625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-626]]

八引發決擇處、 ^4-626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-627]]

九句差別決擇處、 ^4-627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-628]]

十不由功用暫作意時，一切義成決擇處。 ^4-628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-629]]

何等論軌決擇？ ^4-629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-630]]

略有七種： ^4-630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-631]]

一論體、二論處、 ^4-631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-632]]

三論依、四論莊嚴、 ^4-632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-633]]

五論負、六論出離、 ^4-633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-634]]

七論多所作法。 ^4-634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-635]]

第一論體復有六種： ^4-635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-636]]

一言論、二尚論、 ^4-636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-637]]

三諍論、四毀論、 ^4-637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-638]]

五順論、六教論。 ^4-638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-639]]

言論者，謂一切世間語言。 ^4-639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-640]]

尚論者，謂諸世間所隨聞論，世智所尚故。 ^4-640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-641]]

諍論者，謂互相違返所立言論。 ^4-641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-642]]

毀論者，謂更相憤怒發麁惡言。 ^4-642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-643]]

順論者，謂隨順清淨智見所有決擇言論。 ^4-643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-644]]

教論者，謂教導有情心未定者令其心定， ^4-644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-645]]

心已定者令得解脫所有言論。 ^4-645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-646]]

第二論處，謂或於王家、或於執理家、 ^4-646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-647]]

或對淳質堪為量者、或對善伴、 ^4-647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-648]]

或對善解法義沙門婆羅門等而起論端。 ^4-648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-649]]

第三論依，謂依此立論略有二種：一所成立、二能成立。 ^4-649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-650]]

所成立有二種：一自性、二差別。 ^4-650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-651]]

能成立有八種：一立宗、二立因、三立喻、四合、 ^4-651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-652]]

五結、六現量、七比量、八聖教量。 ^4-652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-653]]

所成立自性者，謂我自性或法自性。 ^4-653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-654]]

差別者，謂我差別或法差別。 ^4-654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-655]]

立宗者，謂以所應成自所許義，宣示於他令彼解了。 ^4-655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-656]]

立因者，謂即於所成未顯了義，正說現量可得不可得等信解之相。 ^4-656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-657]]

立喻者，謂以所見邊與未所見，邊和會正說。 ^4-657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-658]]

合者，為引所餘此種類義，令就此法正說理趣。 ^4-658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-659]]

結者，謂到究竟趣所有正說。 ^4-659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-660]]

現量者，謂自正明了無迷亂義。 ^4-660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-661]]

比量者，謂現餘信解。 ^4-661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-662]]

聖教量者，謂不違二量之教。 ^4-662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-663]]

第四論莊嚴， ^4-663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-664]]

謂依論正理而發論端，深為善美，名論莊嚴。 此復六種： ^4-664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-665]]

一善自他宗、 ^4-665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-666]]

二言音圓滿、 ^4-666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-667]]

三無畏、 ^4-667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-668]]

四辯才、 ^4-668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-669]]

五敦肅、 ^4-669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-671]]

六應供。 ^4-671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-672]]

第五論負，謂捨言、言屈、言過。 ^4-672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-673]]

捨言者，謂自發言稱己論失、 ^4-673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-674]]

稱他論德。 ^4-674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-675]]

言屈者， ^4-675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-676]]

謂假託餘事方便而退，或說外事而捨本宗， ^4-676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-677]]

或現忿怒憍慢覆藏等， ^4-677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-678]]

如經廣說。 ^4-678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-679]]

言過者， ^4-679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-680]]

略有九種： ^4-680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-681]]

一雜亂、二麁獷、三不辯了、四無限量、 ^4-681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-682]]

五非義相應、六不應時、七不決定、八不顯了、 ^4-682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-683]]

九不相續。 ^4-683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-684]]

第六論出離， ^4-684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-685]]

謂觀察德失令論出離或復不作。 ^4-685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-686]]

若知敵論非正法器、時眾無德，自無善巧不應興論。 ^4-686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-687]]

若知敵論是正法器、時眾有德，自有善巧方可興論。 ^4-687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-688]]

第七論多所作法，略有三種： 一善達自他宗，由此堪能遍興談論； ^4-688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-689]]

二無畏，由此堪能處一切眾而興論端； ^4-689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-690]]

三辯才，由此堪能於諸問難皆善辯答。 ^4-690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-691]]

復次，若欲自求利益安樂，於諸論軌應善通達，不應與他而興諍論。 ^4-691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-692]]

如薄伽梵於《大乘阿毘達磨經》中說如是言： ^4-692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-693]]

{當正觀察十二處法，} 若諸菩薩欲勤精進修諸善品、 ^4-693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-694]]

欲行真實法隨法行、 欲善攝益一切有情、 ^4-694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-695]]

欲得速證阿耨多羅三藐三菩提者， ^4-695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-696]]

當正觀察十二處法， ^4-696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-697]]

不應與他共興諍論。 ^4-697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-698]]

何等十二？ ^4-698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-699]]

一者宣說證無上義微妙法時，其信解者甚為難得。 ^4-699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-700]]

二者作受教心而請問者，甚為難得。 ^4-700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-701]]

三者時眾賢善觀察德失，甚為難得。 ^4-701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-702]]

四者凡所興論能離六失，甚為難得。 何等為六？ ^4-702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-703]]

謂執著邪宗失、 ^4-703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-704]]

矯亂語失、 ^4-704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-705]]

所作語言不應時失、 ^4-705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-706]]

言退屈失、 ^4-706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-707]]

麁惡語失、 ^4-707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-708]]

心恚怒失。 ^4-708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-709]]

{四者凡所興論能離六失，甚為難得。 } ^4-709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-710]]

五者凡興論時不懷獷毒，甚為難得。 ^4-710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-711]]

六者凡興論時善護他心，甚為難得。 ^4-711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-712]]

七者凡興論時善護定心，甚為難得。 ^4-712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-713]]

八者凡興論時欲令己劣他得勝心，甚為難得。 ^4-713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-714]]

九者己劣他勝心不煩惱，甚為難得。 ^4-714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-715]]

十者心已煩惱得安隱住，甚為難得。 ^4-715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-716]]

十一者既不安住常修善法，甚為難得。 ^4-716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-717]]

十二者於諸善法既不恒修，心未得定能速得定、 ^4-717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-718]]

心已得定能速解脫，甚為難得。 ^4-718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-719]]

何等祕密決擇？ ^4-719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-720]]

謂說餘義名句文身，隱密轉變更顯餘義。 ^4-720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-721]]

如契經言：「逆害於父母，王及二多聞， ^4-721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-722]]

誅國及隨行，是人說清淨。」 ^4-722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-723]]

又契經言：「不信不知恩，斷蜜無容處， ^4-723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-724]]

恒食人所吐，是最上丈夫。」 ^4-724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-725]]

又契經言：「覺不堅為堅，善住於顛倒，極煩惱所惱，得最上菩提。」 ^4-725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-726]]

又契經言：菩薩摩訶薩成就五法，施波羅蜜多速得圓滿。 ^4-726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-727]]

何等為五？ ^4-727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-728]]

一者增益慳悋法性、 ^4-728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-729]]

二者於施有倦、 ^4-729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-730]]

三者憎惡乞求、 ^4-730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-731]]

四者無暫少施、 ^4-731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-732]]

五者遠離於施。 ^4-732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-733]]

又契經言：菩薩摩訶薩成就五法名梵行者，成就第一清淨梵行。 ^4-733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-734]]

何等為五？ ^4-734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-735]]

一者常求以欲離欲、 ^4-735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-736]]

二者捨斷欲法、 ^4-736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-737]]

三者欲貪已生即便堅執、 ^4-737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-738]]

四者怖治欲法、 ^4-738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-739]]

五者三二數貪。 ^4-739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-741]]

何故此論名為「大乘阿毘達磨集」？ ^4-741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-742]]

略有三義，謂等所集故、遍所集故、正所集故。 ^4-742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4049-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-744]]

大乘阿毘達磨集論卷第七 ^4-744
